                                     TO WS-CHK-XTRA-COMM-CODE   
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'A'                TO WS-CHK-XTRA-COMM-CODE  
           END-IF.  
           MOVE 'N'                  TO WS-CHK-PPG-RQRD-IND.
           MOVE RSP06-TOTAL-SAIL-DAYS   
                                     TO WS-CHK-TOTAL-SAIL-DAYS. 
           IF RSP06-FUL-APPLY-AG-COMM-IND (GSUB) > SPACES   
              MOVE RSP06-FUL-APPLY-AG-COMM-IND (GSUB)   
                                     TO WS-CHK-FULL-APLY-AGT-COMM-IND   
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'Y'               TO WS-CHK-FULL-APLY-AGT-COMM-IND   
           END-IF.  
           IF RSP06-EXT-APPLY-AG-COMM-IND (GSUB) > SPACES   
              MOVE RSP06-EXT-APPLY-AG-COMM-IND (GSUB)   
                                     TO WS-CHK-XTRA-APLY-AGT-COMM-IND   
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'Y'               TO WS-CHK-XTRA-APLY-AGT-COMM-IND   
           END-IF.  
           MOVE RSP06-FUL-COMM-COMPARE-PCT (GSUB)   
                                     TO WS-CHK-FULL-COMPARE-PCT.
           MOVE RSP06-CABIN-COMMISSION-AMT  
                                     TO WS-CHK-CABIN-COMM-AMT.  
           MOVE RSP06-FUL-G-COMM-PCT (GSUB) 
                                     TO WS-CHK-FULL-COMM-PCT.   
           MOVE RSP06-FUL-G-COMM-AMT (GSUB) 
                                     TO WS-CHK-FULL-COMM-AMT.   
           MOVE RSP06-EXT-COMM-COMPARE-PCT (GSUB)   
                                     TO WS-CHK-XTRA-COMPARE-PCT.
           MOVE RSP06-EXT-G-COMM-AMT (GSUB) 
                                     TO WS-CHK-XTRA-COMM-AMT.   
           MOVE RSP06-EXT-G-COMM-PCT (GSUB) 
                                     TO WS-CHK-XTRA-COMM-PCT.   
           MOVE RSP06-IBR-SINGLES-PCT   
                                     TO WS-CHK-SINGLES-PCT  

           IF WS-CHK-CPA-CAT-CODE = RSP06-IBR-CATEGORY-CODE 
              MOVE 'N'               TO WS-CHK-UPGRADE-TYPE 
                                        WS-CHK-BUY-GET-UPG-IND  
           ELSE 
              IF WS-CHK-CPA-META-CODE = WS-CHK-CAT-META-CODE
                 MOVE 'M'            TO WS-CHK-UPGRADE-TYPE 
                 MOVE 'N'            TO WS-CHK-BUY-GET-UPG-IND  
              ELSE  
                 MOVE 'G'            TO WS-CHK-UPGRADE-TYPE 
                 MOVE 'Y'            TO WS-CHK-BUY-GET-UPG-IND  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

PRCCMB     MOVE 'B'                  TO WS-CHK-REQ-CABIN-CODE.  

           IF RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM (1:3) = 'TBA' 
PRCCMB*       MOVE 'G'               TO WS-CHK-REQ-CABIN-CODE   
PRCCMB*                                 WS-CHK-CABIN-CAT-CODE   
PRCCMB        MOVE 'G'               TO WS-CHK-CABIN-CAT-CODE   
           ELSE 
PRCCMB*       MOVE 'C'               TO WS-CHK-REQ-CABIN-CODE   
              MOVE 'T'               TO WS-CHK-CABIN-CAT-CODE   
           END-IF.  

           IF (RSP06-FUL-COMM-CODE (GSUB)= 'A' AND  
                 (RSP06-CABIN-COMMISSION-AMT > ZERO OR  
                  RSP06-FUL-G-COMM-AMT (GSUB) > ZERO OR 
                  RSP06-FUL-G-COMM-PCT (GSUB) > ZERO)) OR   
                 (RSP06-EXT-COMM-CODE (GSUB) = 'A' AND  
                  (RSP06-EXT-G-COMM-PCT (GSUB) > ZERO OR
                   RSP06-EXT-G-COMM-AMT (GSUB) > ZERO)) 
              MOVE 'Y'               TO WS-CHK-BONUS-COMM-IND   
           ELSE 
              MOVE 'N'               TO WS-CHK-BONUS-COMM-IND   
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL SUB > RSP06-PAX-CNT
              IF RSP06-PAX-OFFER-ID (PAX-SUB) = RSP06-PAX-OFFER-ID (SUB)
                 EVALUATE SUB   
                    WHEN 1  
                       MOVE RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT (SUB)  
                                        TO WS-CHK-POS-1-CRS-AMT 
                       MOVE RSP06-PAX-PTCHG-AMT (SUB)   
                                        TO WS-CHK-POS-1-NCF-AMT 
                    WHEN 2  
                       MOVE RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT (SUB)  
                                        TO WS-CHK-POS-2-CRS-AMT 
                       MOVE RSP06-PAX-PTCHG-AMT (SUB)   
                                        TO WS-CHK-POS-2-NCF-AMT 
                    WHEN 3  
                       MOVE RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT (SUB)  
                                        TO WS-CHK-POS-3-CRS-AMT 
                       MOVE RSP06-PAX-PTCHG-AMT (SUB)   
                                        TO WS-CHK-POS-3-NCF-AMT 
                    WHEN 4  
                       MOVE RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT (SUB)  
                                        TO WS-CHK-POS-4-CRS-AMT 
                       MOVE RSP06-PAX-PTCHG-AMT (SUB)   
                                        TO WS-CHK-POS-4-NCF-AMT 
                    WHEN 5  
                       MOVE RSP06-PAX-CRUISE-AMT (SUB)  
                                        TO WS-CHK-POS-5-CRS-AMT 
                       MOVE RSP06-PAX-PTCHG-AMT (SUB)   
                                        TO WS-CHK-POS-5-NCF-AMT 
                 END-EVALUATE   
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           MOVE 'N'                  TO WS-CHK-GIFT-IND.
           MOVE 0                    TO AB-SUB. 
           PERFORM VARYING ESUB FROM 1 BY 1 
                     UNTIL ESUB > RSP06-ENTITY-CNT (GSUB)   
              IF RSP06-GIFT-ITEM-CODE (GSUB ESUB) > SPACES  
                 MOVE 'Y'         TO WS-CHK-GIFT-IND
              END-IF
              IF RSP06-BONUS-SAIL-DAYS-PCT (GSUB ESUB) >
                                          WS-CHK-OFF-BNS-SDAY-PCT   
                 MOVE RSP06-BONUS-SAIL-DAYS-PCT (GSUB ESUB) 
                                  TO WS-CHK-OFF-BNS-SDAY-PCT
              END-IF
              ADD RSP06-POS-1-OBC-AMT (GSUB ESUB)   
                                 TO WS-CHK-POS-1-OBC-AMT
              ADD RSP06-POS-2-OBC-AMT (GSUB ESUB)   
                                 TO WS-CHK-POS-2-OBC-AMT
              ADD RSP06-POS-3-OBC-AMT (GSUB ESUB)   
                                 TO WS-CHK-POS-3-OBC-AMT
              ADD RSP06-POS-4-OBC-AMT (GSUB ESUB)   
                                 TO WS-CHK-POS-4-OBC-AMT
              ADD RSP06-POS-5-OBC-AMT (GSUB ESUB)   
                                 TO WS-CHK-POS-5-OBC-AMT
              MOVE RSP06-GIFT-ITEM-CODE (GSUB ESUB) 
                                 TO WS-CHK-GIFT-ITEM-CODE   
              MOVE RSP06-GIFT-BUNDLE-CODE (GSUB ESUB)   
                                 TO WS-CHK-GIFT-BUNDLE-CODE 
              PERFORM 6800-GET-GIFT-OBC THRU 6899-EXIT  
              EVALUATE RSP06-ENTITY-TYPE (GSUB ESUB)
                 WHEN 'RTE' 
                    MOVE RSP06-ENTITY-CODE (GSUB ESUB)  
                                 TO WS-CHK-RATE-CODE
                    MOVE RSP06-ENTITY-OWN-ID (GSUB ESUB)
                                 TO WS-CHK-RATE-OWNER-ID
                    MOVE RSP06-GIFT-ITEM-CODE (GSUB ESUB)   
                                 TO WS-CHK-RATE-GIFT-ITEM-CODE  
                    MOVE RSP06-OBC-ITEM-CODE (GSUB ESUB)
                                 TO WS-CHK-RATE-OBC-ITEM-CODE   
                    MOVE RSP06-BONUS-SAIL-DAYS-PCT (GSUB ESUB)  
                                 TO WS-CHK-RATE-BNS-SDAY-PCT
                    MOVE RSP06-GL-ACCOUNT-NUM (GSUB ESUB)   
                                 TO WS-CHK-RATE-GL-ACC-NUM  
                    MOVE RSP06-GIFT-BUNDLE-CODE (GSUB ESUB) 
                                 TO WS-CHK-RATE-GIFT-BUNDLE-CODE
                 WHEN 'PRO' 
                    MOVE RSP06-ENTITY-CODE (GSUB ESUB)  
                                 TO WS-CHK-PROMO-CODE   
                    MOVE RSP06-ENTITY-OWN-ID (GSUB ESUB)
                                 TO WS-CHK-PROMO-OWNER-ID   
                 WHEN 'PCH' 
                    MOVE RSP06-GIFT-ITEM-CODE (GSUB ESUB)   
                                 TO WS-CHK-PCH-GIFT-ITEM-CODE   
                    MOVE RSP06-OBC-ITEM-CODE (GSUB ESUB)
                                 TO WS-CHK-PCH-OBC-ITEM-CODE
                    MOVE RSP06-BONUS-SAIL-DAYS-PCT (GSUB ESUB)  
                                 TO WS-CHK-PCH-BNS-SDAY-PCT 
                    MOVE RSP06-GL-ACCOUNT-NUM (GSUB ESUB)   
                                 TO WS-CHK-PCH-GL-ACC-NUM   
                    MOVE RSP06-GIFT-BUNDLE-CODE (GSUB ESUB) 
                                 TO WS-CHK-PCH-GIFT-BUNDLE-CODE 
PRCCMB              MOVE RSP06-ENTITY-CODE (GSUB ESUB)  
PRCCMB                           TO WS-CHK-CHOICE-CODE  
PRCCMB              MOVE RSP06-ENTITY-OWN-ID (GSUB ESUB)
PRCCMB                           TO WS-CHK-CHOICE-OWNER-ID  
                 WHEN 'ADB' 
                    IF RSP06-ENTITY-CODE (GSUB ESUB) (1:2) = 'EG'   
                       MOVE RSP06-ENTITY-CODE (GSUB ESUB)   
                                 TO WS-CHK-XTRA-AB-CODE 
                       MOVE RSP06-ENTITY-OWN-ID (GSUB ESUB) 
                                 TO WS-CHK-XTRA-AB-OWNER-ID 
                       MOVE RSP06-GL-ACCOUNT-NUM (GSUB ESUB)
                                 TO WS-CHK-XTRA-AB-GL-ACC-NUM   
                    ELSE
                       ADD 1     TO AB-SUB  
                       MOVE RSP06-ENTITY-CODE (GSUB ESUB)   
                                 TO WS-CHK-AB-CODE (AB-SUB) 
                       MOVE RSP06-ENTITY-OWN-ID (GSUB ESUB) 
                                 TO WS-CHK-AB-OWNER-ID (AB-SUB) 
                       MOVE RSP06-GIFT-ITEM-CODE (GSUB ESUB)
                                 TO WS-CHK-AB-GIFT-ITEM-CODE (AB-SUB)   
                       MOVE RSP06-OBC-ITEM-CODE (GSUB ESUB) 
                                 TO WS-CHK-AB-OBC-ITEM-CODE (AB-SUB)
                       MOVE RSP06-BONUS-SAIL-DAYS-PCT (GSUB ESUB)   
                                 TO WS-CHK-AB-BNS-SDAY-PCT (AB-SUB) 
                       MOVE RSP06-GL-ACCOUNT-NUM (GSUB ESUB)
                                 TO WS-CHK-AB-GL-ACC-NUM (AB-SUB)   
                       MOVE RSP06-GIFT-BUNDLE-CODE (GSUB ESUB)  
                                 TO WS-CHK-AB-GIFT-BUNDLE-CODE (AB-SUB) 
                    END-IF  
              END-EVALUATE  
           END-PERFORM. 

           COMPUTE WS-CHK-SNG-CRS-AMT = (((WS-CHK-POS-1-CRS-AMT +   
                                           WS-CHK-POS-2-CRS-AMT) / 2) * 
                                           WS-CHK-SINGLES-PCT). 

           COMPUTE WS-CHK-SNG-NCF-AMT = (((WS-CHK-POS-1-NCF-AMT +   
                                           WS-CHK-POS-2-NCF-AMT) / 2) * 
                                           WS-CHK-SINGLES-PCT). 

           COMPUTE WS-CHK-SNG-OBC-AMT = (((WS-CHK-POS-1-OBC-AMT +   
                                           WS-CHK-POS-2-OBC-AMT) / 2) * 
                                           WS-CHK-SINGLES-PCT). 

           COMPUTE WS-CHK-SNG-GIFT-OBC-AMT =
                                   (((WS-CHK-POS-1-GIFT-OBC-AMT +   
                                      WS-CHK-POS-2-GIFT-OBC-AMT) / 2) * 
                                      WS-CHK-SINGLES-PCT).  

CALFEE     COMPUTE WS-CHK-SNG-CRS-FARE-FEE-AMT =
CALFEE                         (((WS-CHK-POS-1-CRS-FARE-FEE-AMT +   
CALFEE                           WS-CHK-POS-2-CRS-FARE-FEE-AMT) / 2) *  
CALFEE                           WS-CHK-SINGLES-PCT).   

CALFEE     COMPUTE WS-CHK-SNG-FEE-ADJUST-PCT =  
CALFEE                         (((WS-CHK-POS-1-FEE-ADJUST-PCT + 
CALFEE                            WS-CHK-POS-2-FEE-ADJUST-PCT) / 2) *   
CALFEE                            WS-CHK-SINGLES-PCT).  

           COMPUTE WS-CHK-SNG-CABIN-TOTAL = WS-CHK-SNG-CRS-AMT +
                                            WS-CHK-SNG-NCF-AMT. 

           COMPUTE WS-CHK-DBL-CABIN-TOTAL = WS-CHK-POS-1-CRS-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-2-CRS-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-1-NCF-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-2-NCF-AMT.   

           COMPUTE WS-CHK-TPL-CABIN-TOTAL = WS-CHK-POS-1-CRS-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-2-CRS-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-3-CRS-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-1-NCF-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-2-NCF-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-3-NCF-AMT.   

           COMPUTE WS-CHK-QAD-CABIN-TOTAL = WS-CHK-POS-1-CRS-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-2-CRS-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-3-CRS-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-4-CRS-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-1-NCF-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-2-NCF-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-3-NCF-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-4-NCF-AMT.   

           COMPUTE WS-CHK-QNT-CABIN-TOTAL = WS-CHK-POS-1-CRS-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-2-CRS-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-3-CRS-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-4-CRS-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-5-CRS-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-1-NCF-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-2-NCF-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-3-NCF-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-4-NCF-AMT +  
                                            WS-CHK-POS-5-NCF-AMT.   

           EVALUATE RSP06-PAX-CNT   
              WHEN 1 WHEN 2 
                 MOVE 'YYNNN'      TO WS-CHK-OCC-INDS   
              WHEN 3
                 MOVE 'YYYNN'      TO WS-CHK-OCC-INDS   
              WHEN 4
                 MOVE 'YYYYN'      TO WS-CHK-OCC-INDS   
              WHEN 5
                 MOVE 'YYYYY'      TO WS-CHK-OCC-INDS   
           END-EVALUATE.

980649     PERFORM 8400-INS-SOI-SOP-DATA THRU   
980649             8499-INS-SOI-SOP-EXIT.   

       1510-BUILD-SAIL-RATE.

109253     IF RSP06-IBR-BOOK-STATUS = 'C'   
109253        GO TO 1529-POPULATE-SOI-SOP-EXIT. 

           MOVE ZEROES             TO WS-SR-SUB.

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                               UNTIL SUB > WS-SR-CNT

            IF RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE (PAX-SUB) (1:3) 
                                            = WS-SR-RATE-CODE (SUB) 
               MOVE SUB                     TO WS-SR-SUB
               MOVE WS-SR-CNT               TO SUB  
            END-IF  
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SR-SUB = 0 
              ADD 1                   TO WS-SR-CNT  
              MOVE WS-SR-CNT          TO WS-SR-SUB  

              MOVE LOW-VALUES         TO WS-SR-ENTRY     (WS-SR-SUB)

              MOVE RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE (PAX-SUB) (1:3) 
                TO WS-SR-RATE-CODE (WS-SR-SUB)  
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1 TO WS-SR-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB).

       1520-BUILD-SAIL-RATE-CAT.

           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-SR-CAT-SUB.

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL SUB > WS-SR-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SR-SUB)  

             IF WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE = WS-SR-CAT-CODE (WS-SR-SUB SUB)
                MOVE SUB                             TO WS-SR-CAT-SUB   
                MOVE WS-SR-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SR-SUB) TO SUB 
             END-IF 

           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SR-CAT-SUB = 0 
              ADD 1           TO WS-SR-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SR-SUB)

              MOVE WS-SR-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SR-SUB) TO WS-SR-CAT-SUB 

              MOVE WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE  
                TO WS-SR-CAT-CODE (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB) 
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1 TO WS-SR-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB).  

       1521-BUILD-SAIL-PROMO.   

           IF RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE (PAX-SUB) (4:4) NOT > SPACES 
980649        GO TO 1529-POPULATE-SOI-SOP-EXIT. 
980649*       GO TO 1530-VALIDATE-GUEST-TYPES.  

           MOVE ZEROES             TO WS-SP-SUB.

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                               UNTIL SUB > WS-SP-CNT

            IF RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE (PAX-SUB) (4:4) 
                                            = WS-SP-PROMO-CODE (SUB)
               MOVE SUB                     TO WS-SP-SUB
               MOVE WS-SP-CNT               TO SUB  
            END-IF  
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SP-SUB = 0 
              ADD 1                   TO WS-SP-CNT  
              MOVE WS-SP-CNT          TO WS-SP-SUB  

              MOVE LOW-VALUES         TO WS-SP-ENTRY     (WS-SP-SUB)

              MOVE RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE (PAX-SUB) (4:4) 
                TO WS-SP-PROMO-CODE (WS-SP-SUB) 
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1 TO WS-SP-CABIN-CNT (WS-SP-SUB).

       1522-BUILD-SAIL-PROMO-CAT.   

           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-SP-CAT-SUB.

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL SUB > WS-SP-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SP-SUB)  

             IF WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE = WS-SP-CAT-CODE (WS-SP-SUB SUB)
                MOVE SUB                             TO WS-SP-CAT-SUB   
                MOVE WS-SP-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SP-SUB) TO SUB 
             END-IF 

           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SP-CAT-SUB = 0 
              ADD 1           TO WS-SP-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SP-SUB)

              MOVE WS-SP-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SP-SUB) TO WS-SP-CAT-SUB 

              MOVE WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE  
                TO WS-SP-CAT-CODE (WS-SP-SUB WS-SP-CAT-SUB) 
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1 TO WS-SP-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-SP-SUB WS-SP-CAT-SUB).  

       1523-BUILD-SAL-RTE-PRO.  

           MOVE ZEROES             TO WS-SRP-SUB.   

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                               UNTIL SUB > WS-SRP-CNT   

            IF RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE (PAX-SUB) (1:7) 
                                         = WS-SRP-RATE-PROMO-CODE (SUB) 
               MOVE SUB                     TO WS-SRP-SUB   
               MOVE WS-SRP-CNT              TO SUB  
            END-IF  
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SRP-SUB = 0
              ADD 1                   TO WS-SRP-CNT 
              MOVE WS-SP-CNT          TO WS-SRP-SUB 

              MOVE LOW-VALUES         TO WS-SRP-ENTRY     (WS-SRP-SUB)  

              MOVE RSP06-PAX-OFFER-CODE (PAX-SUB) (1:7) 
                TO WS-SRP-RATE-PROMO-CODE (WS-SRP-SUB)  
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1 TO WS-SRP-CABIN-CNT (WS-SRP-SUB).  

       1524-BUILD-SAL-RTE-PRO-CAT.  

           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-SRP-CAT-SUB.   

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL SUB > WS-SRP-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SRP-SUB)

             IF WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE = WS-SRP-CAT-CODE (WS-SRP-SUB SUB)  
                MOVE SUB                               TO WS-SRP-CAT-SUB
                MOVE WS-SRP-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SRP-SUB) TO SUB   
             END-IF 

           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SRP-CAT-SUB = 0
              ADD 1           TO WS-SRP-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SRP-SUB)  

              MOVE WS-SRP-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SRP-SUB) TO WS-SRP-CAT-SUB  

              MOVE WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE  
                TO WS-SRP-CAT-CODE (WS-SRP-SUB WS-SRP-CAT-SUB)  
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1 TO WS-SRP-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-SRP-SUB WS-SRP-CAT-SUB).   

980649 1529-POPULATE-SOI-SOP-EXIT.  
980649     EXIT.

980649*1530-VALIDATE-GUEST-TYPES.   
980649* 
980649*    EVALUATE RSP06-PAX-AGE-CODE (PAX-SUB)
980649*      WHEN 'Y'   
980649*        IF RSP06-PAX-AGE (PAX-SUB) <= TDP01-MAX-CHILD-AGE
980649*           ADD 1 TO WS-PAX-NBR-CHILDREN  
980649*           IF RSP06-PAX-AGE (PAX-SUB) * 12 <=
980649*                                           TDP01-MAX-INFANT-AGE  
980649*              ADD 1 TO WS-NUM-INFANTS-IN-CAB 
980649*           END-IF
980649*        ELSE 
980649*           ADD 1 TO WS-PAX-NBR-ADULT 
980649*        END-IF   
980649*      WHEN 'M'   
980649*        IF RSP06-PAX-AGE (PAX-SUB) <= TDP01-MAX-INFANT-AGE   
980649*           ADD 1 TO WS-PAX-NBR-CHILDREN  
980649*                    WS-NUM-INFANTS-IN-CAB
980649*        ELSE 
980649*           IF RSP06-PAX-AGE (PAX-SUB) / 12 <= TDP01-MAX-CHILD-AGE
980649*              ADD 1 TO WS-PAX-NBR-CHILDREN   
980649*           ELSE  
980649*              ADD 1 TO WS-PAX-NBR-ADULT  
980649*           END-IF
980649*        END-IF   
980649*      WHEN OTHER 
980649*        IF RSP06-PAX-SEX (PAX-SUB) = 'I' OR  
980649*             RSP06-PAX-AGE (PAX-SUB) <= TDP01-MAX-CHILD-AGE  
980649*           ADD 1 TO WS-PAX-NBR-CHILDREN  
980649*           IF RSP06-PAX-SEX (PAX-SUB) = 'I'  
980649*              ADD 1 TO WS-NUM-INFANTS-IN-CAB 
980649*           END-IF
980649*        ELSE 
980649*           ADD 1 TO WS-PAX-NBR-ADULT 
980649*        END-IF   
980649*    END-EVALUATE.
980649* 
980649*    GO TO 1450-NEXT-PAX-ENTRY.   

       1540-WALK-GST-OFFER-TABLE.   

            ADD 1 TO WS-GST-OFFER-SUB.  

            IF WS-GST-OFFER-SUB > RSP06-GUEST-OFFER-CNT 
               GO TO 1600-COMPARE-PAX-CNTS  
            END-IF. 

            MOVE ZEROES     TO WS-OFFER-COMP-SUB.   

       1550-WALK-OFFER-COMP-TABLE.  

            ADD 1 TO WS-OFFER-COMP-SUB. 

            IF WS-OFFER-COMP-SUB > RSP06-ENTITY-CNT (WS-GST-OFFER-SUB)  
               GO TO 1540-WALK-GST-OFFER-TABLE  
            END-IF. 

            IF RSP06-ENTITY-TYPE
               (WS-GST-OFFER-SUB WS-OFFER-COMP-SUB) NOT = 'ADB' 
               GO TO 1550-WALK-OFFER-COMP-TABLE 
            END-IF. 

            IF (RSP06-GUEST-SEQ-NUM (WS-GST-OFFER-SUB) NOT = 0) AND 
                RSP06-ENTITY-CODE   
               (WS-GST-OFFER-SUB WS-OFFER-COMP-SUB) (1:2) NOT = 'EG'
               GO TO 1550-WALK-OFFER-COMP-TABLE 
            END-IF. 

       1560-BUILD-SAIL-ADB. 

            MOVE ZEROES TO WS-SAB-SUB.  

            PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
                                UNTIL SUB > WS-SAB-CNT  

             IF WS-SAB-CODE (SUB) = 
                  RSP06-ENTITY-CODE (WS-GST-OFFER-SUB WS-OFFER-COMP-SUB)

                 MOVE SUB             TO WS-SAB-SUB 
                 MOVE WS-SAB-CNT      TO SUB
             END-IF 
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SAB-SUB = 0
              ADD 1                   TO WS-SAB-CNT 
              MOVE WS-SAB-CNT         TO WS-SAB-SUB 

              MOVE LOW-VALUES         TO WS-SAB-ENTRY     (WS-SAB-SUB)  

              MOVE RSP06-ENTITY-CODE
                   (WS-GST-OFFER-SUB WS-OFFER-COMP-SUB) 
                                      TO  WS-SAB-CODE (WS-SAB-SUB)  
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1 TO WS-SAB-CABIN-CNT (WS-SAB-SUB).  

       1570-SAIL-ADB-CAT.   

           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-SAB-CAT-SUB.   

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL SUB > WS-SAB-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SAB-SUB)

             IF WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE =   
                            WS-SAB-CAT-CODE (WS-SAB-SUB SUB)

                MOVE SUB TO WS-SAB-CAT-SUB  
                MOVE  WS-SAB-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SAB-SUB) TO SUB  
             END-IF 

           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SAB-CAT-SUB = 0
              ADD 1           TO WS-SAB-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SAB-SUB)  

              MOVE WS-SAB-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SAB-SUB)
                TO WS-SAB-CAT-SUB   

              MOVE WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE  
                TO WS-SAB-CAT-CODE (WS-SAB-SUB WS-SAB-CAT-SUB)  

           END-IF.  

           ADD 1 TO WS-SAB-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-SAB-SUB WS-SAB-CAT-SUB).   

           GO TO 1550-WALK-OFFER-COMP-TABLE.

                  FROM SAIL_CATEGORY
                  WHERE SHIP_CODE     = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE AND
                        SAIL_ID       = :TDP01-SAIL-ID   AND
                        CATEGORY_CODE = :AVAIL-CATEGORY-CODE
PPC470            FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY   
            END-EXEC.   

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 11 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

            MOVE ZEROES TO PAX-SUB  
                           WS-SAIL-CATEGORY-SW. 

       3050-CHECK-NEXT-PAX-ENTRY.   

           ADD 1 TO PAX-SUB.

           IF PAX-SUB > 5   
              GO TO 3100-CHK-SAIL-CATEGORY-UPDATE   
           END-IF.  

           EVALUATE PAX-SUB 

             WHEN 1 
      * This is done as we dont maintain single occupancy counts on 
      * SAIL_CATEGORY table.
                   GO TO 3050-CHECK-NEXT-PAX-ENTRY  
             WHEN 2 
               IF WS-DOUBLE-REQS =  
                                (WCS-GRP-RTB-REQS (WS-CAT-SUB 1)
                               + WCS-GRP-RTB-REQS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB)) 
                  CONTINUE  
               ELSE 
                  MOVE 1  TO WS-SAIL-CATEGORY-SW
                  DISPLAY 'SOLD REQS:' TDP01-SHIP-CODE  
                           TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '  
                           TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ':::'   
                           WS-OFFER-ID ':' AVAIL-CATEGORY-CODE ':'  
                          'OCCUPANCY'  ':' PAX-SUB ':'  
                          'OLD COUNT'  ':' WS-DOUBLE-REQS ':'   
                          'NEW COUNT:' '('  
                           WCS-GRP-RTB-REQS (WS-CAT-SUB 1)  '+' 
                           WCS-GRP-RTB-REQS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) ')'
                           UPON PRINTER 

                  ADD    WCS-GRP-RTB-REQS (WS-CAT-SUB 1)
                         WCS-GRP-RTB-REQS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB)  
                  GIVING WS-DOUBLE-REQS 
               END-IF   

               IF WS-DOUBLE-GTEE-CABIN =
                                WCS-INDV-GTEE-CABS (WS-CAT-SUB 1)   
                              + WCS-INDV-GTEE-CABS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) 
                              + WCS-GRP-GTEE-CABS  (WS-CAT-SUB 1)   
                              + WCS-GRP-GTEE-CABS  (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) 
                  CONTINUE  
               ELSE 
                  MOVE 1  TO WS-SAIL-CATEGORY-SW
                  DISPLAY 'SOLD GTEE:' TDP01-SHIP-CODE  
                          TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '   
                          TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ':::' WS-OFFER-ID ':'
                          AVAIL-CATEGORY-CODE ':' 'OCCUPANCY:' PAX-SUB  
                          ':' 'OLD COUNT' ':'    WS-DOUBLE-GTEE-CABIN   
                          ':' 'NEW COUNT:('   ':'   
                           WCS-INDV-GTEE-CABS (WS-CAT-SUB 1)       '+'  
                           WCS-INDV-GTEE-CABS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) '+'  
                           WCS-GRP-GTEE-CABS  (WS-CAT-SUB 1)       '+'  
                           WCS-GRP-GTEE-CABS  (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) ')'  
                           UPON PRINTER 

                  ADD WCS-INDV-GTEE-CABS (WS-CAT-SUB 1) 
                      WCS-INDV-GTEE-CABS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB)   
                      WCS-GRP-GTEE-CABS  (WS-CAT-SUB 1) 
                      WCS-GRP-GTEE-CABS  (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB)   
                  GIVING  WS-DOUBLE-GTEE-CABIN  
               END-IF   

             WHEN 3 
               IF WS-TRIPLE-REQS = WCS-GRP-RTB-REQS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB)
                  CONTINUE  
               ELSE 
                  MOVE 1  TO WS-SAIL-CATEGORY-SW
                  DISPLAY 'SOLD REQS:'  
                          TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '   
                          TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ':::' WS-OFFER-ID ':'
                          AVAIL-CATEGORY-CODE     ':' 'OCCUPANCY'    ':'
                          PAX-SUB ':' 'OLD COUNT' ':' WS-TRIPLE-REQS ':'
                          'NEW COUNT' ':'   
                          WCS-GRP-RTB-REQS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) 
                          UPON PRINTER  

                  MOVE WCS-GRP-RTB-REQS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) TO 
                       WS-TRIPLE-REQS   
               END-IF   

               IF WS-TRIPLE-GTEE-CABIN =
                                WCS-INDV-GTEE-CABS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) 
                              + WCS-GRP-GTEE-CABS  (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) 
                  CONTINUE  
               ELSE 
                  MOVE 1  TO WS-SAIL-CATEGORY-SW
                  DISPLAY 'SOLD GTEE:'  
                          TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '   
                          TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ':::' WS-OFFER-ID ':'
                          AVAIL-CATEGORY-CODE ':' 'OCCUPANCY' ':'   
                          PAX-SUB ':' 'OLD COUNT'  ':'  
                          WS-TRIPLE-GTEE-CABIN  
                          'NEW COUNT:(' 
                          WCS-INDV-GTEE-CABS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) '+'   
                          WCS-GRP-GTEE-CABS  (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) ')'   
                          UPON PRINTER  

                  ADD     WCS-INDV-GTEE-CABS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB)   
                          WCS-GRP-GTEE-CABS  (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB)   
                  GIVING  WS-TRIPLE-GTEE-CABIN  
               END-IF   

             WHEN 4 
               IF WS-QUAD-REQS = WCS-GRP-RTB-REQS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB)  
                  CONTINUE  
               ELSE 
                  DISPLAY 'SOLD REQS:'  
                          TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '   
                          TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ':::' WS-OFFER-ID ':'
                          AVAIL-CATEGORY-CODE   ':' 'OCCUPANCY:' PAX-SUB
                          ':' 'OLD COUNT' ':' WS-QUAD-REQS ':'  
                          'NEW COUNT' ':'   
                           WCS-GRP-RTB-REQS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB)
                           UPON PRINTER 

                  MOVE 1 TO WS-SAIL-CATEGORY-SW 
                  MOVE WCS-GRP-RTB-REQS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) TO 
                       WS-QUAD-REQS 
               END-IF   

               IF WS-QUAD-GTEE-CABIN =  
                                WCS-INDV-GTEE-CABS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) 
                              + WCS-GRP-GTEE-CABS  (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) 
                  CONTINUE  
               ELSE 
                  MOVE 1  TO WS-SAIL-CATEGORY-SW
                  DISPLAY 'SOLD GTEE:'  
                          TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '   
                          TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ':::' WS-OFFER-ID ':'
                          AVAIL-CATEGORY-CODE ':' 'OCCUPANCY:' PAX-SUB  
                          ':' 'OLD COUNT:' ':' WS-QUAD-GTEE-CABIN ':'   
                          'NEW COUNT:(' 
                           WCS-INDV-GTEE-CABS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) '+'  
                           WCS-GRP-GTEE-CABS  (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) ')'  
                           UPON PRINTER 

                  ADD     WCS-INDV-GTEE-CABS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB)   
                          WCS-GRP-GTEE-CABS  (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB)   
                  GIVING  WS-QUAD-GTEE-CABIN
               END-IF   

             WHEN 5 
               IF WS-QUINT-REQS = WCS-GRP-RTB-REQS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) 
                  CONTINUE  
               ELSE 
                  DISPLAY 'SOLD REQS:'  
                          TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '   
                          TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ':::' WS-OFFER-ID ':'
                          AVAIL-CATEGORY-CODE ':' 'OCCUPANCY:' PAX-SUB  
                          ':' 'OLD COUNT:'    ':' WS-QUINT-REQS ':' 
                          'NEW COUNT'         ':'   
                          WCS-GRP-RTB-REQS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) 
                          UPON PRINTER  

                  MOVE 1 TO WS-SAIL-CATEGORY-SW 
                  MOVE WCS-GRP-RTB-REQS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) TO 
                       WS-QUINT-REQS
               END-IF   

               IF WS-QUINT-GTEE-CABIN = 
                                WCS-INDV-GTEE-CABS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) 
                              + WCS-GRP-GTEE-CABS  (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) 
                  CONTINUE  
               ELSE 
                  MOVE 1  TO WS-SAIL-CATEGORY-SW
                  DISPLAY 'SOLD GTEE:' TDP01-SHIP-CODE  
                          TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '   
                          TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ':::' WS-OFFER-ID ':'
                          AVAIL-CATEGORY-CODE   ':' 'OCCUPANCY:'
                          PAX-SUB ':'   
                          'OLD COUNT' ':' WS-QUINT-GTEE-CABIN ':'   
                          'NEW COUNT:( '
                          WCS-INDV-GTEE-CABS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) '+'   
                          WCS-GRP-GTEE-CABS  (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB) ')'   
                          UPON PRINTER  

                  ADD     WCS-INDV-GTEE-CABS (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB)   
                          WCS-GRP-GTEE-CABS  (WS-CAT-SUB PAX-SUB)   
                  GIVING  WS-QUINT-GTEE-CABIN   
               END-IF   
           END-EVALUATE.

           GO TO 3050-CHECK-NEXT-PAX-ENTRY. 

       3100-CHK-SAIL-CATEGORY-UPDATE.   

           IF WS-TOTAL-REQS = (WS-DOUBLE-REQS   
                               +  WS-TRIPLE-REQS
                               +  WS-QUAD-REQS  
                               +  WS-QUINT-REQS)
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              MOVE 1 TO         WS-SAIL-CATEGORY-SW 

              COMPUTE WS-TOTAL-REQS = (WS-DOUBLE-REQS   
                                    +  WS-TRIPLE-REQS   
                                    +  WS-QUAD-REQS 
                                    +  WS-QUINT-REQS)   

           END-IF.  

           IF WS-TOTAL-GTEE-CABIN =  WS-DOUBLE-GTEE-CABIN   
                                  +  WS-TRIPLE-GTEE-CABIN   
                                  +  WS-QUAD-GTEE-CABIN 
                                  +  WS-QUINT-GTEE-CABIN
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              MOVE 1 TO         WS-SAIL-CATEGORY-SW 

              COMPUTE WS-TOTAL-GTEE-CABIN =  WS-DOUBLE-GTEE-CABIN   
                                          +  WS-TRIPLE-GTEE-CABIN   
                                          +  WS-QUAD-GTEE-CABIN 
                                          +  WS-QUINT-GTEE-CABIN
           END-IF.  

213482     PERFORM VARYING WS-KSUB FROM 1 BY 1  
213482         UNTIL WS-KSUB > 5
213482         IF (WS-KSUB = 2 AND  
213482             WCS-NUM-MAX-CABIN (WS-CAT-SUB WS-KSUB) NOT   
213482                                    = WS-CABIN-DBL)   
213482             MOVE WCS-NUM-MAX-CABIN (WS-CAT-SUB WS-KSUB)  
213482                                                   TO WS-CABIN-DBL
213482             MOVE 1 TO         WS-SAIL-CATEGORY-SW
213482         END-IF   
213482         IF (WS-KSUB = 3 AND  
213482             WCS-NUM-MAX-CABIN (WS-CAT-SUB WS-KSUB) NOT   
213482                                   = WS-CABIN-TRPL)   
213482             MOVE WCS-NUM-MAX-CABIN (WS-CAT-SUB WS-KSUB) TO   
213482                                                    WS-CABIN-TRPL 
213482             MOVE 1 TO         WS-SAIL-CATEGORY-SW
213482         END-IF   
213482         IF (WS-KSUB = 4 AND  
213482             WCS-NUM-MAX-CABIN (WS-CAT-SUB WS-KSUB) NOT = 
213482                                     WS-CABIN-QAUD)   
213482             MOVE WCS-NUM-MAX-CABIN (WS-CAT-SUB WS-KSUB) TO   
213482                                                     WS-CABIN-QAUD
213482             MOVE 1 TO         WS-SAIL-CATEGORY-SW
213482         END-IF   
213482         IF (WS-KSUB = 5 AND  
213482             WCS-NUM-MAX-CABIN (WS-CAT-SUB WS-KSUB) NOT   
213482                                  = WS-CABIN-QUANT)   
213482             MOVE WCS-NUM-MAX-CABIN (WS-CAT-SUB WS-KSUB) TO   
213482                                                    WS-CABIN-QUANT
213482             MOVE 1 TO         WS-SAIL-CATEGORY-SW
213482         END-IF   
213482     END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SAIL-CATEGORY-SW NOT = 1   
              GO TO 3000-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-CATEGORY   
           END-IF.  

       3150-UPDATE-SAIL-CATEGORY.   

           EXEC SQL 
               UPDATE SAIL_CATEGORY 
213482*        SET SOLD_REQS             = :WS-TOTAL-REQS,  
213482         SET  NUM_CABINS_DBL        = :WS-CABIN-DBL,  
213482              NUM_CABINS_TRPL       = :WS-CABIN-TRPL, 
213482              NUM_CABINS_QUAD       = :WS-CABIN-QAUD, 
213482              NUM_CABINS_QUNT       = :WS-CABIN-QUANT,
213482              SOLD_REQS             = :WS-TOTAL-REQS, 
                    SOLD_REQS_DBL         = :WS-DOUBLE-REQS,
                    SOLD_REQS_TRPL        = :WS-TRIPLE-REQS,
                    SOLD_REQS_QUAD        = :WS-QUAD-REQS,  
                    SOLD_REQS_QUNT        = :WS-QUINT-REQS, 
                    SOLD_GTEE_CABIN       = :WS-TOTAL-GTEE-CABIN,   
                    SOLD_GTEE_CABIN_DBL   = :WS-DOUBLE-GTEE-CABIN,  
                    SOLD_GTEE_CABIN_TRPL  = :WS-TRIPLE-GTEE-CABIN,  
                    SOLD_GTEE_CABIN_QUAD  = :WS-QUAD-GTEE-CABIN,
                    SOLD_GTEE_CABIN_QUNT  = :WS-QUINT-GTEE-CABIN

                 WHERE SHIP_CODE          = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE AND
                       SAIL_ID            = :TDP01-SAIL-ID   AND
                       CATEGORY_CODE      = :AVAIL-CATEGORY-CODE
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              DISPLAY 'NOT ABLE TO UPDATE SAIL_CAT FOR REQS/GTEE :::'   
                       TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD '::'
                       AVAIL-CATEGORY-CODE UPON PRINTER 
           END-IF.  

           IF (AUX-INFO-PAGE-CNT > 100) 
               PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT  
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 3000-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-CATEGORY. 

       3200-FETCH-SAIL-RATE.

           IF (AUX-INFO-PAGE-CNT > 0)   
               PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT  
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM 6400-LIST-ALL-RATE-FOLLOWER THRU 6499-EXIT.  

           DISPLAY '<<<<Processing SAIL_RATE>>>>' UPON PRINTER. 

           EXEC SQL DECLARE SAIL_RATE_CUR CURSOR FOR RETENTION  
                SELECT RATE_CODE,   
                       SOLD_CABINS, 
                       SOLD_REQS,   
                       CHILD_FAMILY_SOLD_CABINS,
                       IFNULL (TEAM_CODE, ' '), 
                       CABIN_LINK_IND   
                       FROM SAIL_RATE   
                       WHERE SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE   
                       AND   SAIL_ID   = :TDP01-SAIL-ID 
           END-EXEC.


           EXEC SQL 
                OPEN SAIL_RATE_CUR  
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 13 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

       3250-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-RATE.   

           EXEC SQL 
                FETCH NEXT SAIL_RATE_CUR
                      INTO :WS-RATE-CODE,   
                           :WS-SOLD-COUNT,  
                           :WS-TOTAL-REQS,  
                           :WS-FAMILY-SOLD-CNT, 
                           :WS-TEAM-CODE,   
                           :WS-CAB-LINK-IND 
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 14 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF SQLCODE = 100 
              GO TO 3450-FETCH-SAIL-RATE-CAT
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM 6500-FIND-RTE-FAM-SOLD-CNT THRU 6599-EXIT.   

           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-SR-SUB.

       3300-PROCESS-SAIL-RATE.  

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                           UNTIL SUB > WS-SR-CNT

               IF WS-SR-RATE-CODE (SUB) = WS-RATE-CODE  


                  MOVE SUB       TO WS-SR-SUB   
                  MOVE WS-SR-CNT TO SUB 
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SR-SUB NOT > 0 
              IF (WS-SOLD-COUNT NOT = 0)
                  DISPLAY 'SAILRATE NOT FOUND-UPDATING COUNT TO 0:' 
                           TDP01-SHIP-CODE  ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD
                           ':::' WS-RATE-CODE ':' 'OLD-COUNT:'  
                           WS-SOLD-COUNT UPON PRINTER   

                  MOVE ZEROES TO WS-SOLD-COUNT  
                                 WS-TOTAL-REQS  
                                 WS-FAMILY-SOLD-CNT 

                  GO TO 3350-UPDATE-SAIL-RATE   
              ELSE  
                 GO TO 3400-ADD-SAIL-TEAM-DATA  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-SR-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB)       = WS-SOLD-COUNT AND 
              WS-SR-SOLD-REQS (WS-SR-SUB)       = WS-TOTAL-REQS AND 
              WS-SR-FAMILY-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB) = WS-FAMILY-SOLD-CNT   
              GO TO 3400-ADD-SAIL-TEAM-DATA 
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-SR-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB) NOT = WS-SOLD-COUNT   
              DISPLAY 'SAIL RATE SOLD COUNT INCORRECT:' 
                       TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD '::'
                       WS-RATE-CODE    ':' 'SOLD COUNT:'
                      'OLD COUNT'      ':' WS-SOLD-COUNT
                      'NEW COUNT'      ':' WS-SR-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB)  
                       UPON PRINTER 
              MOVE WS-SR-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB)  TO WS-SOLD-COUNT
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-SR-SOLD-REQS (WS-SR-SUB) NOT = WS-TOTAL-REQS   
              DISPLAY 'SAIL RATE SOLD REQS INCORRECT:'  
                       TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD '::'
                       WS-RATE-CODE    ':' 'SOLD REQS:' 
                      'OLD COUNT '     ':' WS-TOTAL-REQS
                      'NEW COUNT:'     ':' WS-SR-SOLD-REQS (WS-SR-SUB)  
                       UPON PRINTER 
              MOVE WS-SR-SOLD-REQS (WS-SR-SUB)  TO WS-TOTAL-REQS
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-SR-FAMILY-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB)
                                          NOT = WS-FAMILY-SOLD-CNT  
              DISPLAY 'SAIL RATE CHILD FAMILY SOLD COUNT INCORRECT:'
                       TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD '::'
                       WS-RATE-CODE    ':' 'SOLD REQS:' 
                      'OLD COUNT '     ':' WS-FAMILY-SOLD-CNT   
                      'NEW COUNT:'     ':'  
                       WS-SR-FAMILY-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB)   
                       UPON PRINTER 
              MOVE WS-SR-FAMILY-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB)   
                TO WS-FAMILY-SOLD-CNT   
           END-IF.  

       3350-UPDATE-SAIL-RATE.   

           EXEC SQL 
               UPDATE SAIL_RATE 
               SET SOLD_CABINS              = :WS-SOLD-COUNT,   
                   SOLD_REQS                = :WS-TOTAL-REQS,   
                   CHILD_FAMILY_SOLD_CABINS = :WS-FAMILY-SOLD-CNT   
               WHERE
                   SHIP_CODE      = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE AND
                   SAIL_ID        = :TDP01-SAIL-ID   AND
                   RATE_CODE      = :WS-RATE-CODE   
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              DISPLAY 'NOT ABLE TO UPDATE SAIL_RATE FOR REQS OR CABINS:'
                       TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD  ':'
                       WS-RATE-CODE UPON PRINTER
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF (AUX-INFO-PAGE-CNT > 100) 
               PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT  
           END-IF.  

       3400-ADD-SAIL-TEAM-DATA. 

           IF WS-TEAM-CODE NOT > SPACES 
              GO TO 3250-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-RATE   
           END-IF.  

           EXEC SQL 
              SELECT 'Y' INTO :WS-EXIST-IND 
                FROM SAIL_RATE_LEADER   
               WHERE SHIP_CODE       = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE 
                 AND SAIL_ID         = :TDP01-SAIL-ID   
                 AND PARENT_CODE     = :WS-TEAM-CODE
                 AND CONTROL_TYPE    = 'TEAM'   
                 AND CHILD_RATE_CODE = :WS-RATE-CODE
PRCCMB           FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY 
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           ELSE 
              IF SQLCODE = 100  
                 EXEC SQL INSERT INTO SAIL_RATE_LEADER COLUMNS  
                          (SHIP_CODE       = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE   
                          ,SAIL_ID         = :TDP01-SAIL-ID 
                          ,PARENT_CODE     = :WS-TEAM-CODE  
                          ,CONTROL_TYPE    = 'TEAM' 
                          ,CHILD_RATE_CODE = :WS-RATE-CODE) 
                 END-EXEC   
                 IF SQLCODE < 0 
                    GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR   
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           EXEC SQL 
               UPDATE SAIL_RATE_CATEGORY
                  SET TEAM_CODE = :WS-TEAM-CODE 
                     ,CABIN_LINK_IND = :WS-CAB-LINK-IND 
                WHERE SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE  
                  AND SAIL_ID   = :TDP01-SAIL-ID
                  AND RATE_CODE = :WS-RATE-CODE 
                  AND (TEAM_CODE <> :WS-TEAM-CODE   
                    OR CABIN_LINK_IND <> :WS-CAB-LINK-IND)  
           END-EXEC 

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF   

           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-ST-SUB.

           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                                  UNTIL WS-SUB > WS-ST-CNT  

             IF WS-ST-CODE (WS-SUB) = WS-TEAM-CODE  
                MOVE WS-SUB        TO WS-ST-SUB 
                MOVE WS-ST-CNT     TO WS-SUB
             END-IF 
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-ST-SUB > 0 
              COMPUTE WS-ST-CABIN-CNT (WS-ST-SUB)   
                    = WS-ST-CABIN-CNT (WS-ST-SUB) + WS-SOLD-COUNT   
           ELSE 
              ADD 1              TO WS-ST-CNT   
              MOVE WS-ST-CNT     TO WS-ST-SUB   
              MOVE WS-TEAM-CODE  TO WS-ST-CODE      (WS-ST-SUB) 
              MOVE WS-SOLD-COUNT TO WS-ST-CABIN-CNT (WS-ST-SUB) 
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 3250-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-RATE. 

       3450-FETCH-SAIL-RATE-CAT.

           EXEC SQL 
                CLOSE SAIL_RATE_CUR 
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              DISPLAY 'ERROR WHILE CLOSING SAIL_RATE_CUR CURSOR'
              UPON PRINTER  
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF (AUX-INFO-PAGE-CNT > 0)   
               PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT  
           END-IF.  

           DISPLAY '<<<<Processing SAIL_RATE_CAT>>>>' UPON PRINTER. 
           EXEC SQL DECLARE SAIL_RATE_CAT_CUR CURSOR FOR RETENTION  
                SELECT RATE_CODE,   
                       CATEGORY_CODE,   
                       SOLD_CABINS, 
                       SOLD_REQS,   
                       CHILD_FAMILY_SOLD_CABINS,
                       IFNULL(TEAM_CODE, ' ')   
                       FROM SAIL_RATE_CATEGORY  
                       WHERE SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE   
                       AND   SAIL_ID   = :TDP01-SAIL-ID 
           END-EXEC.

           EXEC SQL 
                OPEN SAIL_RATE_CAT_CUR  
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 16 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

       3500-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-RATE-CAT.   

           EXEC SQL 
                FETCH NEXT SAIL_RATE_CAT_CUR
                      INTO :WS-RATE-CODE,   
                           :WS-CATEGORY-CODE,   
                           :WS-SOLD-COUNT,  
                           :WS-TOTAL-REQS,  
                           :WS-FAMILY-SOLD-CNT, 
                           :WS-TEAM-CODE
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 17 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF SQLCODE = 100 
              GO TO 3200-FETCH-SAIL-PROMO   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE ZEROES TO  WS-SR-SUB
                           WS-SR-CAT-SUB.   

       3550-PROCESS-SAIL-RATE-CAT.  
           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                           UNTIL SUB > WS-SR-CNT

             IF WS-SR-RATE-CODE (SUB)  = WS-RATE-CODE   

                PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                               UNTIL WS-SUB > WS-SR-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (SUB) 

                  IF WS-SR-CAT-CODE (SUB WS-SUB)= WS-CATEGORY-CODE  
                     MOVE WS-SUB                      TO WS-SR-CAT-SUB  
                     MOVE WS-SR-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (SUB)   TO WS-SUB 
                  END-IF
                END-PERFORM 

                MOVE SUB         TO WS-SR-SUB   
                MOVE WS-SR-CNT   TO SUB 
             END-IF 
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SR-CAT-SUB NOT > 0 
              IF (WS-SOLD-COUNT NOT = 0)
                  DISPLAY 'SAIL-RATE-CAT NOT FOUND-UPDATING CNT TO 0:'  
                           TDP01-SHIP-CODE  ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD
                           ':::' WS-RATE-CODE ':' WS-CATEGORY-CODE ':'  
                           'OLD-COUNTS'     ':' WS-SOLD-COUNT      ':'  
                           WS-TOTAL-REQS        UPON PRINTER

                 MOVE ZEROES TO WS-SOLD-COUNT   
                                WS-TOTAL-REQS   
                                WS-FAMILY-SOLD-CNT  

                 GO TO 3600-UPDATE-SAIL-RATE-CAT
              ELSE  
               GO TO 3650-ADD-SAIL-TEAM-CAT-DATA
           END-IF.  

           IF (WS-SOLD-COUNT =  
                      WS-SR-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB)) AND
              (WS-TOTAL-REQS =  
238724                WS-SR-CAT-SOLD-REQS (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB))
238724*               WS-SR-CAT-SOLD-REQS (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB)) AND
238724*        (WS-FAMILY-SOLD-CNT =
238724*            WS-SR-CAT-FAMILY-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB))

               GO TO 3650-ADD-SAIL-TEAM-CAT-DATA
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-SR-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB) 
                                                     NOT = WS-SOLD-COUNT
              DISPLAY 'SAIL RATE SOLD COUNT INCORRECT:' 
                       TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD '::'
                       WS-RATE-CODE    ':' WS-CATEGORY-CODE ':' 
                      'OLD COUNT'      ':' WS-SOLD-COUNT    ':' 
                      'NEW COUNT'      ':'  
                       WS-SR-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB)
                       UPON PRINTER 

              MOVE WS-SR-CAT-CABIN-CNT    (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB) 
                TO WS-SOLD-COUNT
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-SR-CAT-SOLD-REQS (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB) 
                                                    NOT = WS-TOTAL-REQS 
              DISPLAY 'SAIL RATE SOLD REQS INCORRECT:'  
                       TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD  ':'
                       WS-RATE-CODE    ':' WS-CATEGORY-CODE ':' 
                      'OLD COUNT'      ':' WS-TOTAL-REQS    ':' 
                      'NEW COUNT'      ':'  
                       WS-SR-CAT-SOLD-REQS (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB)
                       UPON PRINTER 

              MOVE WS-SR-CAT-SOLD-REQS (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB)
                TO WS-TOTAL-REQS
           END-IF.  

238724*    IF WS-SR-CAT-FAMILY-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB)  
238724*                                        NOT = WS-FAMILY-SOLD-CNT 
238724*       DISPLAY 'SAIL RATE CHILD FAMILY SOLD COUNT INCORRECT:'
238724*                TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD '::'
238724*                WS-RATE-CODE    ':' WS-CATEGORY-CODE ':' 
238724*               'OLD COUNT'      ':' WS-FAMILY-SOLD-CNT    ':'
238724*               'NEW COUNT'      ':'  
238724*                WS-SR-CAT-FAMILY-CABIN-CNT   
238724*                                 (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB)   
238724*                UPON PRINTER 
238724* 
238724*       MOVE WS-SR-CAT-FAMILY-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB) 
238724*         TO WS-FAMILY-SOLD-CNT   
238724*    END-IF.  

       3600-UPDATE-SAIL-RATE-CAT.   
           EXEC SQL 
               UPDATE SAIL_RATE_CATEGORY
               SET SOLD_CABINS              = :WS-SOLD-COUNT,   
                   SOLD_REQS                = :WS-TOTAL-REQS,   
                   CHILD_FAMILY_SOLD_CABINS = :WS-FAMILY-SOLD-CNT   
               WHERE
                   SHIP_CODE      = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE AND
                   SAIL_ID        = :TDP01-SAIL-ID   AND
                   RATE_CODE      = :WS-RATE-CODE    AND
                   CATEGORY_CODE  = :WS-CATEGORY-CODE   
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              DISPLAY 'NOT ABLE TO UPDATE SAIL_RATE_CAT FOR :::'
              TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ':'  
              WS-RATE-CODE    ':' WS-CATEGORY-CODE  
              UPON PRINTER  
           END-IF.  

           IF (AUX-INFO-PAGE-CNT > 100) 
               PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT  
           END-IF.  

       3650-ADD-SAIL-TEAM-CAT-DATA. 

           IF WS-TEAM-CODE NOT > SPACES 
              GO TO 3500-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-RATE-CAT   
           END-IF.  

           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-ST-SUB 
                          WS-ST-CAT-SUB.

           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                                  UNTIL WS-SUB > WS-ST-CNT  
            IF WS-TEAM-CODE = WS-ST-CODE (WS-SUB)   
               PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                                UNTIL SUB > WS-ST-CAT-ENTRY-CNT(WS-SUB) 
                 IF WS-CATEGORY-CODE = WS-ST-CAT-CODE (WS-SUB SUB)  
                    MOVE SUB                       TO WS-ST-CAT-SUB 
                    MOVE WS-ST-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SUB)  TO SUB   
                 END-IF 
              END-PERFORM   
              MOVE WS-SUB      TO WS-ST-SUB 
              MOVE WS-ST-CNT   TO WS-SUB
            END-IF  
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-ST-CAT-SUB > 0 
              COMPUTE WS-ST-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-ST-SUB WS-ST-CAT-SUB) 
                    = WS-ST-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-ST-SUB WS-ST-CAT-SUB) 
                    + WS-SOLD-COUNT 
           ELSE 
              ADD 1                 TO WS-ST-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-ST-SUB)  
              MOVE WS-ST-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-ST-SUB)  
                TO WS-ST-CAT-SUB
              MOVE WS-CATEGORY-CODE 
                TO WS-ST-CAT-CODE      (WS-ST-SUB WS-ST-CAT-SUB)
              MOVE WS-SOLD-COUNT
                TO WS-ST-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-ST-SUB WS-ST-CAT-SUB)
           END-IF.  
           GO TO 3500-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-RATE-CAT. 

       3200-FETCH-SAIL-PROMO.   

           EXEC SQL 
                CLOSE SAIL_RATE_CAT_CUR 
           END-EXEC 
           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              DISPLAY 'ERROR WHILE CLOSING SAIL_RATE_CAT_CUR CURSOR'
              UPON PRINTER  
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF (AUX-INFO-PAGE-CNT > 0)   
               PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT

                       SOLD_CABINS

           END-EXEC.

           END-EXEC.

                           :WS-SOLD-COUNT

           END-IF.

                           UNTIL SUB > WS-SP-CNT

               IF WS-SP-PROMO-CODE (SUB) = WS-PROMO-CODE

                  MOVE SUB       TO WS-SP-SUB   
                  MOVE WS-SP-CNT TO SUB 
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SP-SUB NOT > 0 
              IF (WS-SOLD-COUNT NOT = 0)
                  DISPLAY 'SAILPROMO NOT FOUND-UPDATING COUNT TO 0:'
                           TDP01-SHIP-CODE  ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD
                           ':::' WS-PROMO-CODE ':' 'OLD-COUNT:' 
                           WS-SOLD-COUNT UPON PRINTER

                  GO TO 3350-UPDATE-SAIL-PROMO  
              ELSE  
                 GO TO 3250-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-PROMO   
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-SP-CABIN-CNT (WS-SP-SUB) = WS-SOLD-COUNT   
              GO TO 3250-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-PROMO  
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-SP-CABIN-CNT (WS-SP-SUB) NOT = WS-SOLD-COUNT   
              DISPLAY 'SAIL PROMO SOLD COUNT INCORRECT:'
                       TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD '::'
                       WS-PROMO-CODE    ':' 'SOLD COUNT:'   
                      'OLD COUNT'      ':' WS-SOLD-COUNT
                      'NEW COUNT'      ':' WS-SP-CABIN-CNT (WS-SP-SUB)  
                       UPON PRINTER 
              MOVE WS-SP-CABIN-CNT (WS-SP-SUB)  TO WS-SOLD-COUNT
           END-IF.
