PRCCMB*                                              NOT = WS-SOLD-COUNT
PRCCMB*       DISPLAY 'SAIL RATE SOLD COUNT INCORRECT:' 
PRCCMB*                TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD '::'
PRCCMB*                WS-PROMO-CODE    ':' WS-CATEGORY-CODE ':'
PRCCMB*               'OLD COUNT'      ':' WS-SOLD-COUNT    ':' 
PRCCMB*               'NEW COUNT'      ':'  
PRCCMB*                WS-SRP-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-SRP-SUB WS-SRP-CAT-SUB) 
PRCCMB*                UPON PRINTER 

PRCCMB*       MOVE WS-SRP-CAT-CABIN-CNT    (WS-SRP-SUB WS-SRP-CAT-SUB)  
PRCCMB*         TO WS-SOLD-COUNT
PRCCMB*    END-IF.  

PRCCMB*3600-UPDATE-SAL-RTE-PRO-CAT. 
PRCCMB*    EXEC SQL 
PRCCMB*        UPDATE SAIL_RATE_PROMO_CAT   
PRCCMB*        SET SOLD_CABINS    = :WS-SOLD-COUNT  
PRCCMB*        WHERE
PRCCMB*            SHIP_CODE      = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE AND
PRCCMB*            SAIL_ID        = :TDP01-SAIL-ID   AND
PRCCMB*            RATE_CODE      = :WS-RATE-CODE    AND
PRCCMB*            PROMOTION_CODE     = :WS-PROMO-CODE   AND
PRCCMB*            CATEGORY_CODE  = :WS-CATEGORY-CODE   
PRCCMB*    END-EXEC.

PRCCMB*    IF SQLCODE < 0   
PRCCMB*       DISPLAY 'NOT ABLE TO UPDATE SAIL_RATE_PROMO_CAT FOR :::'  
PRCCMB*       TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ':'  
PRCCMB*       WS-PROMO-CODE    ':' WS-CATEGORY-CODE 
PRCCMB*       UPON PRINTER  
PRCCMB*    END-IF.  

PRCCMB*    IF (AUX-INFO-PAGE-CNT > 100) 
PRCCMB*        PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT  
PRCCMB*    END-IF.  

       3700-FETCH-SAIL-ADB. 

PRCCMB*    EXEC SQL 
PRCCMB*         CLOSE SAIL_RATE_PROMO_CAT_CUR   
PRCCMB*    END-EXEC 
PRCCMB*    IF SQLCODE < 0   
PRCCMB*       DISPLAY 'ERROR WHILE CLOSING SAIL_RATE_PROMO_CAT_CUR' 
PRCCMB*               ' CURSOR' UPON PRINTER
PRCCMB*       GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
PRCCMB*    END-IF.  

PRCCMB*    IF (AUX-INFO-PAGE-CNT > 0)   
PRCCMB*        PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT  
PRCCMB*    END-IF.  
           DISPLAY '<<<<Processing SAIL_ADB>>>>' UPON PRINTER.  
           EXEC SQL DECLARE SAIL_ADB_CUR CURSOR FOR RETENTION   
                SELECT ADD_BENEFIT_CODE,
                       SOLD_CABINS  
                       FROM SAIL_ADD_BNFT   
                       WHERE SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE   
                       AND   SAIL_ID   = :TDP01-SAIL-ID 
                       AND   ADD_BENEFIT_TYPE IN ('S','E')  
           END-EXEC.

           EXEC SQL 
                OPEN SAIL_ADB_CUR   
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 19 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

       3750-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-ADB.

           EXEC SQL 
                FETCH NEXT SAIL_ADB_CUR 
                      INTO :WS-ADB-CODE,
                           :WS-SOLD-COUNT   
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 20 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF SQLCODE = 100 
              GO TO 3900-FETCH-SAIL-ADB-CAT 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-SAB-SUB.   

       3800-PROCESS-SAIL-ADB.   

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                           UNTIL SUB > WS-SAB-CNT   

              IF WS-SAB-CODE (SUB) = WS-ADB-CODE
                 MOVE SUB         TO WS-SAB-SUB 
                 MOVE WS-SAB-CNT  TO SUB
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SAB-SUB NOT > 0
              IF (WS-SOLD-COUNT NOT = 0)
                  DISPLAY 'SAILADB NOT FOUND- UPDATING COUNT TO 0:' 
                           TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD 
                           ':' WS-ADB-CODE ':' 'OLD-COUNTS'  ':'
                           WS-SOLD-COUNT UPON PRINTER   

                   MOVE ZEROES TO WS-SOLD-COUNT 
                   GO TO 3850-UPDATE-SAIL-ADB   
              ELSE  
                 GO TO 3750-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-ADB 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-SAB-CABIN-CNT (WS-SAB-SUB) = WS-SOLD-COUNT 
              GO TO 3750-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-ADB
           END-IF.  

           DISPLAY 'SAIL ADB SOLD COUNT INCORRECT:::'   
                    TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ':'
                    WS-ADB-CODE     ':' 'OLD COUNT:' WS-SOLD-COUNT  
                   'NEW COUNT'      ':' WS-SAB-CABIN-CNT (WS-SAB-SUB)   
                    UPON PRINTER.   

           MOVE WS-SAB-CABIN-CNT (WS-SAB-SUB)  TO WS-SOLD-COUNT.

       3850-UPDATE-SAIL-ADB.

           EXEC SQL 
               UPDATE SAIL_ADD_BNFT 
               SET SOLD_CABINS            = :WS-SOLD-COUNT  
                   WHERE SHIP_CODE        = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE AND
                         SAIL_ID          = :TDP01-SAIL-ID   AND
                         ADD_BENEFIT_CODE = :WS-ADB-CODE
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              DISPLAY 'NOT ABLE TO UPDATE SAIL_ADB FOR :::' 
              TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ':'  
              WS-ADB-CODE         UPON PRINTER  
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF (AUX-INFO-PAGE-CNT > 100) 
               PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT  
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 3750-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-ADB.  

       3900-FETCH-SAIL-ADB-CAT. 

           EXEC SQL 
                CLOSE SAIL_ADB_CUR  
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 21 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           DISPLAY '<<<<Processing SAIL_ADB_CAT>>>>' UPON PRINTER.  
           EXEC SQL DECLARE SAIL_ADB_CAT_CUR CURSOR FOR RETENTION   
                SELECT ADD_BENEFIT_CODE,
                       CATEGORY_CODE,   
                       SOLD_CABINS  
                       FROM SAIL_ADD_BNFT_CAT   
                       WHERE SHIP_CODE        = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE
                         AND SAIL_ID          = :TDP01-SAIL-ID  
                         AND ADD_BENEFIT_TYPE = 'S' 
           END-EXEC.

           EXEC SQL 
                OPEN SAIL_ADB_CAT_CUR   
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 23 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

       3950-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-ADB-CAT.

           EXEC SQL 
                FETCH NEXT SAIL_ADB_CAT_CUR 
                      INTO :WS-ADB-CODE,
                           :WS-CATEGORY-CODE,   
                           :WS-SOLD-COUNT   
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 24 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF SQLCODE = 100 
              GO TO 4100-FETCH-SAIL-TEAM
           END-IF.  

           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-SAB-SUB
                          WS-SAB-CAT-SUB.   

       4000-PROCESS-SAIL-ADB-CAT.   

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                           UNTIL SUB > WS-SAB-CNT   

            IF WS-SAB-CODE (SUB) = WS-ADB-CODE  

               PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                               UNTIL WS-SUB > WS-SAB-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (SUB)

                  IF WS-SAB-CAT-CODE (SUB WS-SUB) = WS-CATEGORY-CODE
                     MOVE WS-SUB                      TO WS-SAB-CAT-SUB 
                     MOVE WS-SAB-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (SUB)  TO WS-SUB 
                  END-IF
               END-PERFORM  

               MOVE SUB         TO WS-SAB-SUB   
               MOVE WS-SAB-CNT  TO SUB  
            END-IF  
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SAB-CAT-SUB NOT > 0
              IF (WS-SOLD-COUNT NOT = 0)
                  DISPLAY 'SAIL-ADB-CAT NOT FOUND-UPDATING CNT TO 0:'   
                           TDP01-SHIP-CODE  ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD
                           ':::' WS-ADB-CODE   ':' 'OLD-COUNTS' ':' 
                            WS-SOLD-COUNT       UPON PRINTER

                 MOVE ZEROES TO WS-SOLD-COUNT   
                 GO TO 4050-UPDATE-SAIL-ADB-CAT 
              ELSE  
                 GO TO 3950-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-ADB-CAT 
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-SOLD-COUNT =   
                        WS-SAB-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-SAB-SUB WS-SAB-CAT-SUB)
              GO TO 3950-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-ADB-CAT
           END-IF.  

           DISPLAY 'SAIL ADBCAT SOLD COUNT INCORRECT::::'   
                   TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ':' 
                   WS-ADB-CODE     ':' 'OLD COUNT'     ':'  
                   WS-SOLD-COUNT   ':' 'NEW COUNT'     ':'  
                   WS-SAB-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-SAB-SUB WS-SAB-CAT-SUB) 
                   UPON PRINTER.

           MOVE WS-SAB-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-SAB-SUB WS-SAB-CAT-SUB)
             TO WS-SOLD-COUNT.  

       4050-UPDATE-SAIL-ADB-CAT.

           EXEC SQL 
               UPDATE SAIL_ADD_BNFT_CAT 
               SET    SOLD_CABINS       = :WS-SOLD-COUNT
               WHERE  SHIP_CODE         = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE  AND 
                      SAIL_ID           = :TDP01-SAIL-ID    AND 
                      ADD_BENEFIT_CODE  = :WS-ADB-CODE      AND 
                      CATEGORY_CODE     = :WS-CATEGORY-CODE 
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              DISPLAY 'NOT ABLE TO UPDATE SAIL_ADD_BNFT_CAT FOR :::'
                       TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ':' 
                       WS-ADB-CODE     ':' WS-CATEGORY-CODE 
                       UPON PRINTER 
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF (AUX-INFO-PAGE-CNT > 100) 
               PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT  
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 3950-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-ADB-CAT.  

       4100-FETCH-SAIL-TEAM.

           EXEC SQL 
                CLOSE SAIL_ADB_CAT_CUR  
           END-EXEC.

           IF (AUX-INFO-PAGE-CNT > 0)   
               PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT  
           END-IF.  

           DISPLAY '<<<<Processing SAIL_TEAM>>>>' UPON PRINTER. 
           EXEC SQL DECLARE SAIL_TEAM_CUR CURSOR FOR RETENTION  
                SELECT TEAM_CODE,   
                       SOLD_CABINS  
                       FROM SAIL_TEAM   
                       WHERE SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE   
                       AND   SAIL_ID   = :TDP01-SAIL-ID 
           END-EXEC.

           EXEC SQL 
                OPEN SAIL_TEAM_CUR  
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 26 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

       4150-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-TEAM.   

           EXEC SQL 
                FETCH NEXT SAIL_TEAM_CUR
                      INTO :WS-TEAM-CODE,   
                           :WS-SOLD-COUNT   
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 27 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF SQLCODE = 100 
              GO TO 4300-FETCH-SAIL-TEAM-CAT
           END-IF.  

       4200-PROCESS-SAIL-TEAM.  

           EXEC SQL 
              SELECT IFNULL(SUM(RTE.SOLD_CABINS +   
                                RTE.CHILD_FAMILY_SOLD_CABINS),0)
                INTO :WS-TEAM-SOLD-CABINS   
              FROM SAIL_RATE_LEADER SRL 
              JOIN SAIL_RATE RTE ON 
                   RTE.SHIP_CODE = SRL.SHIP_CODE AND
                   RTE.SAIL_ID = SRL.SAIL_ID AND
                   RTE.RATE_CODE = SRL.CHILD_RATE_CODE  
              WHERE SRL.SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE AND
                    SRL.SAIL_ID = :TDP01-SAIL-ID AND
                    SRL.PARENT_CODE = :WS-TEAM-CODE AND 
                    SRL.CONTROL_TYPE  = 'TEAM'  
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE = 100 
              MOVE 0 TO WS-TEAM-SOLD-CABINS 
           END-IF.  

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 28 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-TEAM-SOLD-CABINS = WS-SOLD-COUNT   
              GO TO 4150-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-TEAM   
           END-IF.  

           DISPLAY 'SAIL TEAM SOLD COUNT INCORRECT:::'  
                    TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ':'
                   'OLD COUNT'      ':' WS-SOLD-COUNT   
                   'NEW COUNT'      ':' WS-TEAM-SOLD-CABINS 
                    UPON PRINTER.   

       4250-UPDATE-SAIL-TEAM.   

           EXEC SQL 
               UPDATE SAIL_TEAM 
               SET SOLD_CABINS            = :WS-TEAM-SOLD-CABINS,   
                   LAST_UPDATE_DATETIME   = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(0)
                   WHERE SHIP_CODE        = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE AND
                         SAIL_ID          = :TDP01-SAIL-ID   AND
                         TEAM_CODE        = :WS-TEAM-CODE   
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              DISPLAY 'NOT ABLE TO UPDATE SAIL_TEAM FOR :'  
                       TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ':' 
                       WS-TEAM-CODE        UPON PRINTER 
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF (AUX-INFO-PAGE-CNT > 100) 
               PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT  
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 4150-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-TEAM. 

       4300-FETCH-SAIL-TEAM-CAT.

           EXEC SQL 
                CLOSE SAIL_TEAM_CUR 
           END-EXEC.

           IF (AUX-INFO-PAGE-CNT > 0)   
               PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT  
           END-IF.  

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 32 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           DISPLAY '<<<<Processing SAIL_TEAM_CAT>>>>' UPON PRINTER. 
           EXEC SQL DECLARE SAIL_TEAM_CAT_CUR CURSOR FOR RETENTION  
                SELECT TEAM_CODE,   
                       CATEGORY_CODE,   
                       SOLD_CABINS  
                       FROM SAIL_TEAM_CATEGORY  
                       WHERE SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE   
                         AND SAIL_ID   = :TDP01-SAIL-ID 
           END-EXEC.

           EXEC SQL 
                OPEN SAIL_TEAM_CAT_CUR  
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 30 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  


       4350-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-TEAM-CAT.   

           EXEC SQL 
                FETCH NEXT SAIL_TEAM_CAT_CUR
                      INTO :WS-TEAM-CODE,   
                           :WS-CATEGORY-CODE,   
                           :WS-SOLD-COUNT   
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 31 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF SQLCODE = 100 
              GO TO 5000-UPDATE-SAIL-RECORD 
           END-IF.  

       4400-PROCESS-SAIL-TEAM-CAT.  

           EXEC SQL 
              SELECT IFNULL(
                 SUM(RTE.SOLD_CABINS + RTE.CHILD_FAMILY_SOLD_CABINS),0) 
                INTO :WS-TEAM-SOLD-CABINS   
              FROM SAIL_RATE_LEADER SRL 
              JOIN SAIL_RATE_CATEGORY RTE ON
                   RTE.SHIP_CODE = SRL.SHIP_CODE AND
                   RTE.SAIL_ID = SRL.SAIL_ID AND
                   RTE.RATE_CODE = SRL.CHILD_RATE_CODE AND  
                   RTE.CATEGORY_CODE = :WS-CATEGORY-CODE
              WHERE SRL.SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE AND
                    SRL.SAIL_ID = :TDP01-SAIL-ID AND
                    SRL.PARENT_CODE = :WS-TEAM-CODE AND 
                    SRL.CONTROL_TYPE  = 'TEAM'  
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE = 100 
              MOVE 0 TO WS-TEAM-SOLD-CABINS 
           END-IF.  

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 33 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-SOLD-COUNT =  WS-TEAM-SOLD-CABINS  
              GO TO 4350-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-TEAM-CAT   
           END-IF.  

           DISPLAY 'SAIL TEAMCAT SOLD COUNT INCORRECT:' 
                    TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ':'
                    WS-TEAM-CODE    ':' 'OLD COUNT'    ':'  
                    WS-SOLD-COUNT   ':'  'NEW COUNT'   ':'  
                    WS-TEAM-SOLD-CABINS 
                    UPON PRINTER.   

       4450-UPDATE-SAIL-TEAM-CAT.   

           EXEC SQL 
               UPDATE SAIL_TEAM_CATEGORY
               SET    SOLD_CABINS       = :WS-TEAM-SOLD-CABINS, 
                   LAST_UPDATE_DATETIME = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(0)  
               WHERE  SHIP_CODE         = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE  AND 
                      SAIL_ID           = :TDP01-SAIL-ID    AND 
                      TEAM_CODE         = :WS-TEAM-CODE     AND 
                      CATEGORY_CODE     = :WS-CATEGORY-CODE 
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              DISPLAY 'NOT ABLE TO UPDATE SAIL_TEAM_CAT FOR :::'
                       TDP01-SHIP-CODE ':' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ':' 
                       WS-TEAM-CODE    ':' WS-CATEGORY-CODE 
                       UPON PRINTER 
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF (AUX-INFO-PAGE-CNT > 100) 
               PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT  
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 4350-FETCH-NEXT-SAIL-TEAM-CAT. 

       5000-UPDATE-SAIL-RECORD. 

           EXEC SQL 
                CLOSE SAIL_TEAM_CAT_CUR 
           END-EXEC.

           IF (AUX-INFO-PAGE-CNT > 0)   
               PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT  
           END-IF.  

           DISPLAY '<<<<Processing SAILING>>>>' UPON PRINTER.   
           MOVE ZERO TO WS-SAIL-MOD-SW. 

           ADD WSI-NUM-EST-PAX TO WSI-PAX-CNT.  

           IF WSI-PAX-CNT  NOT = TDP01-SOLD-GUEST-CNT   
              MOVE 1 TO WS-SAIL-MOD-SW  
              DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '   
                      TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD  
                      ' PAX COUNT = ' WSI-PAX-CNT   
                      '  SAIL = ' TDP01-SOLD-GUEST-CNT  UPON PRINTER
              MOVE WSI-PAX-CNT  TO N18-SIGNED   
              MOVE N18-UNSIGNED TO TDP01-SOLD-GUEST-CNT 
           END-IF.  

           IF WSI-NUM-EST-PAX NOT = TDP01-ESTIMATED-GUESTS  
              MOVE 1 TO WS-SAIL-MOD-SW  
              DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '   
                      TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD  
                      ' GRP EST PAX  = ' WSI-NUM-EST-PAX  '  SAIL = '   

                      TDP01-ESTIMATED-GUESTS  UPON PRINTER  
              MOVE WSI-NUM-EST-PAX TO N18-SIGNED
              MOVE N18-UNSIGNED    TO TDP01-ESTIMATED-GUESTS
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-SAIL-GRP-REQ-CNT NOT = TDP01-SOLD-REQS 
              MOVE 1 TO WS-SAIL-MOD-SW  
              DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '   
                      TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD  
                      ' GRP REQ CNT = ' WS-SAIL-GRP-REQ-CNT 
                      '  SAIL1 = ' TDP01-SOLD-REQS UPON PRINTER 
              MOVE WS-SAIL-GRP-REQ-CNT TO TDP01-SOLD-REQS   
           END-IF.  

           IF WSI-CABIN-NET NOT = TDP01-CABIN-NET   
              MOVE 1 TO WS-SAIL-MOD-SW  
              DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '   
                      TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD  
                      ' CABIN-NET     = ' WSI-CABIN-NET 
                   '  SAIL = '       TDP01-CABIN-NET UPON PRINTER   
              MOVE WSI-CABIN-NET TO TDP01-CABIN-NET 
           END-IF.  

PRCCMB     IF  WSI-RSRV-AFF-NET-CABINS NOT = TDP01-RSRV-AFF-NET-CABINS  
PRCCMB         MOVE 1                       TO WS-SAIL-MOD-SW   
PRCCMB         DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '  
PRCCMB             TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD 
PRCCMB             ' RSRV-AFF-NET-CABINS = ' WSI-RSRV-AFF-NET-CABINS
PRCCMB             '  SAIL = ' TDP01-RSRV-AFF-NET-CABINS
PRCCMB             UPON PRINTER 
PRCCMB         MOVE WSI-RSRV-AFF-NET-CABINS TO TDP01-RSRV-AFF-NET-CABINS
PRCCMB     END-IF.  

           IF TDP01-DIN-SAIL-ID = TDP01-SAIL-ID AND 
              TDP01-SAIL-DIN-CNT > 1
              CONTINUE  
           ELSE 
              PERFORM TDP01-RET-ALL-SDIN THRU   
                      TDP01-RET-ALL-SDIN-EXIT   
              IF TDP01-SDIN-SQLERR  
                 GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL SUB > 6
              IF WSI-DINING-PAX-CNT (SUB) NOT = 
                    TDP01-GUESTS-ASSIGNED-CNT (SUB) 
                 MOVE 1 TO WS-SAIL-MOD-SW   
                 DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '
                         TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD   
                          ' Gtee Dining Time Code: "'   
                         TDP01-DINING-TIME-CODE (SUB) '" Pax = '
                         WSI-DINING-PAX-CNT (SUB) ' Sail = '
                         TDP01-GUESTS-ASSIGNED-CNT (SUB) UPON PRINTER   
                 MOVE WSI-DINING-PAX-CNT (SUB) TO   
                      TDP01-GUESTS-ASSIGNED-CNT (SUB)   
              END-IF

              IF TDP01-MAX-GUESTS (SUB) < TDP01-GUESTS-ASSIGNED-CNT(SUB)
                 DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '
                         TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD   
                         ' Gtee Dining Time Code: "'
                         TDP01-DINING-TIME-CODE (SUB) '" Max = '
                         TDP01-MAX-GUESTS (SUB) ' Pax = '   
                         TDP01-GUESTS-ASSIGNED-CNT (SUB) UPON PRINTER   
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           MOVE 'N'                       TO WS-OPT-ACT 
                                             WS-MOT-ACT.

           MOVE TDP01-BTH-OPTION-DAYS     TO WS-HOLD-OPTION-DAYS.   
           MOVE TDP01-BTH-OPTION-HOURS    TO WS-HOLD-OPTION-HOURS.  
           MOVE TDP01-BTH-MAIL-DAYS       TO WS-HOLD-OPT-MAIL-DAYS. 

           IF PARAM-DAY-PRIOR = ZEROS   
              GO TO 5250-CHECK-SAIL1-MODIFY 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE PARAM-DAY-PRIOR TO DPP02-NUMBER-DAYS.   

           IF DPP02-SYSTEM-HH > 21  
              ADD 1 TO DPP02-NUMBER-DAYS.   

           MOVE DPP02-SYSTEM-DATE TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE.   

           PERFORM DPP10-ADVANCE-YYYYMMDD-DATE. 

           IF TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD < DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE OR 
                 TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD > DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE 
              GO TO 5250-CHECK-SAIL1-MODIFY 
           END-IF.  

           IF PARAM-OPTION-DAY > 0  
              IF TDP01-BTH-OPTION-DAYS > 0  
                 IF TDP01-BTH-OPTION-DAYS <= PARAM-OPTION-DAY   
                    GO TO 5100-UPDATE-MAIL-DAYS 
                 END-IF 
              ELSE  
                 GO TO 5100-UPDATE-MAIL-DAYS
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              GO TO 5050-CHECK-OPTION-HR
           END-IF.  

           MOVE PARAM-OPTION-DAY       TO WS-HOLD-OPTION-DAYS.  
           MOVE TDP01-BTH-OPTION-DAYS  TO WS-OPT-FROM-DUR.  
           MOVE PARAM-OPTION-DAY       TO WS-OPT-TO-DUR.
           MOVE ' Days'                TO WS-OPT-FROM-TYPE  
                                          WS-OPT-TO-TYPE.   
           MOVE 'Y'                    TO WS-OPT-ACT.   

           GO TO 5100-UPDATE-MAIL-DAYS. 

       5050-CHECK-OPTION-HR.
           IF PARAM-OPTION-HR > 0   
              IF TDP01-BTH-OPTION-DAYS > 0  
                 MOVE PARAM-OPTION-HR        TO WS-HOLD-OPTION-HOURS
                 MOVE 0                      TO WS-HOLD-OPTION-DAYS 
                 MOVE TDP01-BTH-OPTION-DAYS  TO WS-OPT-FROM-DUR 
                 MOVE PARAM-OPTION-HR        TO WS-OPT-TO-DUR   
                 MOVE ' Days'                TO WS-OPT-FROM-TYPE
                 MOVE ' Hours'               TO WS-OPT-TO-TYPE  
                 MOVE 'Y'                    TO WS-OPT-ACT  
              ELSE  
                 IF TDP01-BTH-OPTION-HOURS > PARAM-OPTION-HR
                    MOVE PARAM-OPTION-HR TO WS-HOLD-OPTION-HOURS
                    MOVE 0               TO WS-HOLD-OPTION-DAYS 
                    MOVE TDP01-BTH-OPTION-HOURS TO WS-OPT-FROM-DUR  
                    MOVE PARAM-OPTION-HR TO WS-OPT-TO-DUR   
                    MOVE ' Hours'        TO WS-OPT-FROM-TYPE
                                            WS-OPT-TO-TYPE  
                    MOVE 'Y'             TO WS-OPT-ACT. 

       5100-UPDATE-MAIL-DAYS.   
           IF PARAM-MAIL-DAY > 0
              IF TDP01-BTH-OPTION-DAYS > 0  
                 IF TDP01-BTH-MAIL-DAYS <= PARAM-OPTION-DAY 
                    GO TO 5200-CHECK-FOR-OPT-MAIL-UPD   
                 END-IF 
              ELSE  
                 GO TO 5200-CHECK-FOR-OPT-MAIL-UPD  
           ELSE 
              GO TO 5200-CHECK-FOR-OPT-MAIL-UPD.

           MOVE PARAM-MAIL-DAY           TO WS-HOLD-OPT-MAIL-DAYS   
           MOVE TDP01-BTH-MAIL-DAYS      TO WS-MOT-FROM-DUR.
           MOVE PARAM-MAIL-DAY           TO WS-MOT-TO-DUR.  
           MOVE ' Days'                  TO WS-MOT-FROM-TYPE
                                            WS-MOT-TO-TYPE. 
           MOVE 'Y'                      TO WS-MOT-ACT. 

           GO TO 5200-CHECK-FOR-OPT-MAIL-UPD.   

       5200-CHECK-FOR-OPT-MAIL-UPD. 

           IF (WS-HOLD-OPTION-DAYS NOT = TDP01-BTH-OPTION-DAYS) OR  
                 (WS-HOLD-OPTION-HOURS NOT = TDP01-BTH-OPTION-HOURS) OR 
                 (WS-HOLD-OPT-MAIL-DAYS NOT = TDP01-BTH-MAIL-DAYS)  

              MOVE 1                      TO WS-SNR-MOD-SW  
                                             WS-SAIL-MOD-SW 
              MOVE WS-HOLD-OPTION-DAYS    TO TDP01-BTH-OPTION-DAYS  
              MOVE WS-HOLD-OPTION-HOURS   TO TDP01-BTH-OPTION-HOURS 
              MOVE WS-HOLD-OPT-MAIL-DAYS  TO TDP01-BTH-MAIL-DAYS

121789     IF  ((WS-HOLD-OPTION-DAYS * 24)  
121789                        + WS-HOLD-OPTION-HOURS) > 0 AND   
121789         ((WS-HOLD-OPTION-DAYS * 24)  
121789                     + WS-HOLD-OPTION-HOURS) <
121789         ((TDP01-BTH-EXTENSION-DAYS * 24) 
121789                     + TDP01-BTH-EXTENSION-HOURS) 
121789         MOVE WS-HOLD-OPTION-DAYS 
121789                                 TO   
121789                                 TDP01-BTH-EXTENSION-DAYS 
121789         MOVE WS-HOLD-OPTION-HOURS
121789                                 TO   
121789                                 TDP01-BTH-EXTENSION-HOURS
121789         MOVE 1                  TO WS-SAIL-MOD-SW
121789     END-IF.  

       5250-CHECK-SAIL1-MODIFY. 
           IF (PARAM-TEST-IND = 'T') OR WS-SAIL-MOD-SW NOT = 1  
               GO TO 5300-CHECK-SAIL-NOTES  
           END-IF.  

           DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '  
                   TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD 
                   ' SAILING RECORD MODIFIED' UPON PRINTER. 

           EXEC SQL 
                UPDATE SAILING  
                SET SOLD_GUEST_CNT         = :TDP01-SOLD-GUEST-CNT, 
                    ESTIMATED_GUESTS       = :TDP01-ESTIMATED-GUESTS,   
                    SOLD_REQS              = :TDP01-SOLD-REQS,  
                    CABIN_NET              = :TDP01-CABIN-NET,  
PRCCMB              RSRV_AFF_NET_CABINS    = :TDP01-RSRV-AFF-NET-CABINS,
                    BTH_OPTION_DAYS        = :TDP01-BTH-OPTION-DAYS,
                    BTH_OPTION_HOURS       = :TDP01-BTH-OPTION-HOURS,   
                    BTH_MAIL_DAYS          = :TDP01-BTH-MAIL-DAYS,  
                    BTH_EXTENSION_DAYS     = :TDP01-BTH-EXTENSION-DAYS, 
                    BTH_EXTENSION_HOURS    = :TDP01-BTH-EXTENSION-HOURS,
                    UPDATE_DATETIME        =  GETUTCDATE()  
                WHERE SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE  
                AND   SAIL_ID   = :TDP01-SAIL-ID
           END-EXEC.
           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 54 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  
           IF SQLCODE = 100 
              CONTINUE  
           END-IF.  

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
              UNTIL SUB > 6 
              EXEC SQL  
                 UPDATE  SAIL_DINING
                    SET  GUESTS_ASSIGNED_CNT =  
                                   :TDP01-GUESTS-ASSIGNED-CNT(SUB)  
                  WHERE  SHIP_CODE  = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE  
                  AND    SAIL_ID    = :TDP01-SAIL-ID
                  AND    DINING_TIME_CODE   
                                    = :TDP01-DINING-TIME-CODE(SUB)  
              END-EXEC  
              IF SQLCODE < 0
                 MOVE 55 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
                 DISPLAY 'ERROR WHILE UPDATING SAIL_DINING- '   
                 TDP01-SHIP-CODE '/'  TDP01-SAIL-ID '/' 
                 TDP01-DINING-TIME-CODE(SUB) UPON PRINTER   
                 GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR  
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT. 

           MOVE 'Y'         TO WS-UPDATE-IND.   

       5300-CHECK-SAIL-NOTES.   

           IF WS-SNR-MOD-SW = ZERO  
              GO TO 0300-FETCH-NEXT-SAILING 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'DPBCSU'               TO GRP05-SHTR1-USERID.

           IF TDP01-BTH-OPTION-DAYS = ZEROES
              IF TDP01-BTH-OPTION-HOURS > ZEROES
                 MOVE TDP01-BTH-OPTION-HOURS TO WS-OPTION-COMM  
                 MOVE 'H' TO WS-OPTION-COMM-TYPE
              ELSE  
                 NEXT SENTENCE  
           ELSE 
              MOVE TDP01-BTH-OPTION-DAYS TO WS-OPTION-COMM  
              MOVE 'D' TO WS-OPTION-COMM-TYPE.  

           IF WS-OPTION-COMM-1 = '0'
              MOVE SPACES TO WS-OPTION-COMM-1.  

           MOVE SPACES                 TO WS-MAIL-COMMENT.  

           IF TDP01-BTH-MAIL-DAYS = ZEROES  
              MOVE TDP01-BTH-MAIL-DAYS TO WS-MAIL-COMM  
              MOVE 'H'                 TO WS-MAIL-COMM-TYPE 
              MOVE 'PAP'               TO WS-MAIL-COMMENT   
           ELSE 
              IF TDP01-BTH-MAIL-DAYS > 6
                 MOVE 'C'              TO WS-MAIL-COMMENT   
              ELSE  
                 MOVE TDP01-BTH-MAIL-DAYS TO WS-MAIL-COMM   
                 MOVE 'D' TO WS-MAIL-COMM-TYPE  
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-MAIL-COMM-1 = '0'  
              MOVE SPACES TO WS-MAIL-COMM-1.

           MOVE SPACES TO WS-INDV-OPT-MAIL-TEXT.

           STRING ' ' DELIMITED SIZE
                  WS-OPTION-COMM-1 WS-OPTION-COMM-2 
                  WS-OPTION-COMM-TYPE DELIMITED ' ' 
                  ' + ' DELIMITED SIZE  
                  WS-MAIL-COMM-1 WS-MAIL-COMM-2 
                  WS-MAIL-COMM-TYPE DELIMITED ' '   
                  INTO WS-INDV-OPT-MAIL-TEXT.   

           MOVE WS-INDV-MAIN-SAIL-NOTES TO WS-NOTE-TEXT.

           IF WS-NOTE-TEXT = SPACES 
              GO TO 0300-FETCH-NEXT-SAILING 
           END-IF.  

           DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '  
                   TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD 
                   ' SAILING NOTES MODIFIED' UPON PRINTER.  

           MOVE WS-NOTE-TEXT  TO TDP01-INDV-NOTE.   

           EXEC SQL 
              UPDATE SAILING
                     SET INDV_NOTE = :TDP01-INDV-NOTE   
              WHERE  SHIP_CODE     = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE   
              AND    SAIL_ID       = :TDP01-SAIL-ID 
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 56 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           MOVE 'Y'              TO WS-UPDATE-IND.  

           DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '  
                   TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD 
                   ' SAIL1-NOTE-REC MODIFIED' UPON PRINTER. 


           IF WS-OPT-ACT = 'Y' AND (PARAM-TEST-IND NOT = 'T')   
              MOVE 'OPT'            TO GRP05-SHTR1-ACTIVITY-CODE
              STRING WS-OPT-FROM-DUR WS-OPT-FROM-TYPE DELIMITED '  '
                     INTO WS-FROM-TEXT  
              STRING WS-OPT-TO-DUR WS-OPT-TO-TYPE DELIMITED '  '
                     INTO WS-TO-TEXT
              STRING 'Option Time:'  WS-FROM-TEXT ' to '  WS-TO-TEXT
                     DELIMITED SIZE INTO GRP05-SHTR1-ACTIVITY-COMMENT   
              MOVE 'CSU'            TO GRP05-SHTR1-METHOD-OF-CHG
              MOVE 'SLG'             TO GRP05-SHTR1-ENTITY-TYPE 
              MOVE 'SAIL'            TO GRP05-SHTR1-ENTITY-CODE 
              PERFORM GRP05-CREATE-SAILING-HISTORY. 

           IF WS-MOT-ACT = 'Y' AND (PARAM-TEST-IND NOT = 'T')   
              MOVE 'MOT'            TO GRP05-SHTR1-ACTIVITY-CODE
              STRING WS-MOT-FROM-DUR WS-MOT-FROM-TYPE DELIMITED '  '
                     INTO WS-FROM-TEXT  
              STRING WS-MOT-TO-DUR  WS-MOT-TO-TYPE  DELIMITED '  '  
                     INTO WS-TO-TEXT
              STRING 'Mail Option Time:' WS-FROM-TEXT  DELIMITED '  '   
                     ' to ' DELIMITED SIZE  
                     WS-TO-TEXT DELIMITED '  '  
                     INTO GRP05-SHTR1-ACTIVITY-COMMENT  
              MOVE 'CSU'            TO GRP05-SHTR1-METHOD-OF-CHG
              MOVE 'SLG'             TO GRP05-SHTR1-ENTITY-TYPE 
              MOVE 'SAIL'            TO GRP05-SHTR1-ENTITY-CODE 

              PERFORM GRP05-CREATE-SAILING-HISTORY. 

           MOVE 'Main Sail Note was:' TO GRP05-SHTR1-ACTIVITY-COMMENT.  

           MOVE 'SNC'                TO GRP05-SHTR1-ACTIVITY-CODE.  
           MOVE 'CSU'                TO GRP05-SHTR1-METHOD-OF-CHG.  
           MOVE 'SLG'             TO GRP05-SHTR1-ENTITY-TYPE.   
           MOVE 'SAIL'            TO GRP05-SHTR1-ENTITY-CODE.   

           IF (PARAM-TEST-IND NOT = 'T')
              PERFORM GRP05-CREATE-SAILING-HISTORY. 

           IF WS-SNR-NOTE-TEXT2 > SPACES AND (PARAM-TEST-IND NOT = 'T') 
              MOVE 'SNC'             TO GRP05-SHTR1-ACTIVITY-CODE   
              MOVE WS-SNR-NOTE-TEXT2 TO GRP05-SHTR1-ACTIVITY-COMMENT
              MOVE 'CSU'             TO GRP05-SHTR1-METHOD-OF-CHG   
              MOVE 'SLG'             TO GRP05-SHTR1-ENTITY-TYPE 
              MOVE 'SAIL'            TO GRP05-SHTR1-ENTITY-CODE 

              PERFORM GRP05-CREATE-SAILING-HISTORY. 

           IF WS-SNR-NOTE-TEXT3 > SPACES AND (PARAM-TEST-IND NOT = 'T') 
              MOVE 'SNC'             TO GRP05-SHTR1-ACTIVITY-CODE   
              MOVE WS-SNR-NOTE-TEXT3 TO GRP05-SHTR1-ACTIVITY-COMMENT
              MOVE 'CSU'             TO GRP05-SHTR1-METHOD-OF-CHG   
              MOVE 'SLG'             TO GRP05-SHTR1-ENTITY-TYPE 
              MOVE 'SAIL'            TO GRP05-SHTR1-ENTITY-CODE 

              PERFORM GRP05-CREATE-SAILING-HISTORY. 

           IF WS-SNR-NOTE-TEXT4 > SPACES AND (PARAM-TEST-IND NOT = 'T') 
              MOVE 'SNC'             TO GRP05-SHTR1-ACTIVITY-CODE   
              MOVE WS-SNR-NOTE-TEXT4 TO GRP05-SHTR1-ACTIVITY-COMMENT
              MOVE 'CSU'             TO GRP05-SHTR1-METHOD-OF-CHG   
              MOVE 'SLG'             TO GRP05-SHTR1-ENTITY-TYPE 
              MOVE 'SAIL'            TO GRP05-SHTR1-ENTITY-CODE 

              PERFORM GRP05-CREATE-SAILING-HISTORY. 

           IF WS-SNR-NOTE-TEXT5 > SPACES AND (PARAM-TEST-IND NOT = 'T') 
              MOVE 'SNC'             TO GRP05-SHTR1-ACTIVITY-CODE   
              MOVE WS-SNR-NOTE-TEXT5 TO GRP05-SHTR1-ACTIVITY-COMMENT
              MOVE 'CSU'             TO GRP05-SHTR1-METHOD-OF-CHG   
              MOVE 'SLG'             TO GRP05-SHTR1-ENTITY-TYPE 
              MOVE 'SAIL'            TO GRP05-SHTR1-ENTITY-CODE 

              PERFORM GRP05-CREATE-SAILING-HISTORY. 

           IF WS-SNR-NOTE-TEXT6 > SPACES AND (PARAM-TEST-IND NOT = 'T') 
              MOVE 'SNC'             TO GRP05-SHTR1-ACTIVITY-CODE   
              MOVE WS-SNR-NOTE-TEXT6 TO GRP05-SHTR1-ACTIVITY-COMMENT
              MOVE 'CSU'             TO GRP05-SHTR1-METHOD-OF-CHG   
              MOVE 'SLG'             TO GRP05-SHTR1-ENTITY-TYPE 
              MOVE 'SAIL'            TO GRP05-SHTR1-ENTITY-CODE 

              PERFORM GRP05-CREATE-SAILING-HISTORY. 

           IF (PARAM-TEST-IND NOT = 'T')
               PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT  
           END-IF.  

           GO TO 0300-FETCH-NEXT-SAILING.   

980649 5500-BUILD-BOOKED-IND.   

980649     MOVE ZERO                     TO WS-SOLD-SUB.

980649     PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
980649         UNTIL SUB > WS-SOLD-CNT  
980649         IF  WS-OFFER-ID = WS-SOLD-OFFER-ID (SUB) 
980649             MOVE SUB              TO WS-SOLD-SUB 
980649             MOVE WS-SOLD-CNT      TO SUB 
980649         END-IF   
980649     END-PERFORM. 

980649     IF  WS-SOLD-SUB = ZERO   
980649         ADD 1                     TO WS-SOLD-CNT 
980649         IF  WS-SOLD-CNT > 5000   
980649             DISPLAY 'SOLD TABLE OVERFLOW' UPON PRINTER   
980649             GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR
980649         END-IF   
980649         MOVE WS-SOLD-CNT          TO WS-SOLD-SUB 
980649         MOVE LOW-VALUES           TO WS-SOLD-ENTRY (WS-SOLD-SUB) 
980649         MOVE WS-OFFER-ID          TO WS-SOLD-OFFER-ID
980649                                                    (WS-SOLD-SUB) 
980649     END-IF.  

980649     IF  WS-SOLD-BOOKED-IND (WS-SOLD-SUB) NOT = 'Y'   
980649         MOVE 'Y'                  TO WS-SOLD-BOOKED-IND  
980649                                                    (WS-SOLD-SUB) 
980649     END-IF.  

980649     IF  WS-CPA-CAT-CODE > SPACES 
980649         MOVE WS-CPA-CAT-CODE      TO WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE 
980649     ELSE 
980649         MOVE WS-CATEGORY-CODE     TO WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE 
980649     END-IF.  

980649     MOVE ZEROES                   TO WS-SOLD-CAT-SUB.

980649     PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
980649         UNTIL SUB > WS-SOLD-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SOLD-SUB)  
980649         IF  WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE =
980649             WS-SOLD-CPA-CAT-CODE (WS-SOLD-SUB SUB)     AND   
980649             WS-CATEGORY-CODE =   
980649             WS-SOLD-CAT-CODE     (WS-SOLD-SUB SUB)     AND   
980649             WS-CABIN-TYPE =  
980649             WS-SOLD-CABIN-TYPE   (WS-SOLD-SUB SUB)   
980649             MOVE SUB              TO WS-SOLD-CAT-SUB 
980649             MOVE WS-SOLD-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SOLD-SUB) 
980649                                   TO SUB 
980649         END-IF   
980649     END-PERFORM. 

980649     IF  WS-SOLD-CAT-SUB = 0  
980649         ADD 1                     TO WS-SOLD-CAT-ENTRY-CNT   
980649                                                     (WS-SOLD-SUB)
980649         IF  WS-SOLD-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SOLD-SUB) > 1000   
980649             DISPLAY 'SOLD CAT TBL OVERFLOW' UPON PRINTER 
980649             GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR
980649         END-IF   
980649         MOVE WS-SOLD-CAT-ENTRY-CNT                  (WS-SOLD-SUB)
980649                                   TO WS-SOLD-CAT-SUB 
980649         MOVE WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE  TO WS-SOLD-CPA-CAT-CODE
980649                                     (WS-SOLD-SUB WS-SOLD-CAT-SUB)
980649         MOVE WS-CATEGORY-CODE     TO WS-SOLD-CAT-CODE
980649                                     (WS-SOLD-SUB WS-SOLD-CAT-SUB)
980649         MOVE WS-CABIN-TYPE        TO WS-SOLD-CABIN-TYPE  
980649                                     (WS-SOLD-SUB WS-SOLD-CAT-SUB)
980649     END-IF.  

980649     IF  WS-SOLD-CAT-BOOKED-IND   
980649         (WS-SOLD-SUB WS-SOLD-CAT-SUB) NOT = 'Y'  
980649         MOVE 'Y'                  TO WS-SOLD-CAT-BOOKED-IND  
980649                                     (WS-SOLD-SUB WS-SOLD-CAT-SUB)
980649     END-IF.  

980649     IF  (WS-LOGIC-FLOW = 'I'                ) AND
980649         (RSP06-IBR-CANCEL-DATE = ZEROES     ) AND
980649         (RSP06-IBR-CABIN-NUM (1:1) NOT = 'W')
980649         ADD 1                     TO WS-SOLD-CABIN-CNT   
980649                                                     (WS-SOLD-SUB)
980649                                      WS-SOLD-CAT-CABIN-CNT   
980649                                     (WS-SOLD-SUB WS-SOLD-CAT-SUB)
980649  
980649     END-IF.  

980649 5500-EXIT.

       6000-CREATE-SOI-DATA.

           IF AVL-CAB-RATE-CODE <= SPACES   
              MOVE '~' TO AVL-CAB-RATE-CODE.

           IF AVL-CAB-BOOK-NUM <= SPACES
              MOVE '~' TO AVL-CAB-BOOK-NUM. 

           IF AVL-CAB-WHEELCHAIR-IND = 'N' OR   
                 AVL-CAB-WHEELCHAIR-IND < SPACES
              MOVE SPACES TO AVL-CAB-WHEELCHAIR-IND.

           IF (AVL-CAB-CABIN-STATUS NOT = 'B')  
              MOVE '~' TO AVL-CAB-BOOK-NUM. 

           IF AVL-CAB-CABIN-STATUS <= SPACES
              MOVE SPACES TO AVL-CAB-CABIN-STATUS.  

PRCCMB*    EVALUATE AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY
PRCCMB     EVALUATE AVL-CAB-NORMAL-CAPACITY 
              WHEN 2
                 MOVE WS-SC-SPOILAGE-DBL-CODE     TO WS-SPOILAGE-CODE   
                 MOVE WS-SC-SPOILAGE-DBL-CODE-NI  TO WS-SPOILAGE-CODE-NI
              WHEN 3
                 MOVE WS-SC-SPOILAGE-TRPL-CODE    TO WS-SPOILAGE-CODE   
                 MOVE WS-SC-SPOILAGE-TRPL-CODE-NI TO WS-SPOILAGE-CODE-NI
              WHEN 4
                 MOVE WS-SC-SPOILAGE-QUAD-CODE    TO WS-SPOILAGE-CODE   
                 MOVE WS-SC-SPOILAGE-QUAD-CODE-NI TO WS-SPOILAGE-CODE-NI
              WHEN 5
                 MOVE WS-SC-SPOILAGE-QUNT-CODE    TO WS-SPOILAGE-CODE   
                 MOVE WS-SC-SPOILAGE-QUNT-CODE-NI TO WS-SPOILAGE-CODE-NI
           END-EVALUATE.

           IF WS-SPOILAGE-CODE-NI = WS-NULL-IND 
              MOVE ' ' TO WS-SPOILAGE-CODE. 

           IF AVL-CAB-CONNECT-CABIN-NUM > SPACES
              PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                      UNTIL SUB > AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY  
                 IF WS-SPOILAGE-CODE-NI = WS-NULL-IND OR
                      (WS-SPOILAGE-CODE = 'M' AND   
                        SUB = AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY) OR  
                      (WS-SPOILAGE-CODE = 'N' AND   
                       SUB >= AVL-CAB-NORMAL-CAPACITY) OR   
                      (WS-SPOILAGE-CODE = '1' AND   
                       SUB >= AVL-CAB-NORMAL-CAPACITY - 1) OR   
                      (WS-SPOILAGE-CODE = '2' AND   
                       SUB >= AVL-CAB-NORMAL-CAPACITY - 2)  
                    ADD 1 TO WCT-CNT
                    MOVE AVL-CAB-CABIN-NUM TO WCT-CABIN-NUM (WCT-CNT)   
                    MOVE AVL-CAB-CONNECT-CABIN-NUM TO   
                         WCT-CONNECT-CABIN-NUM (WCT-CNT)
                    MOVE AVAIL-CATEGORY-CODE TO 
                         WCT-CATEGORY-CODE (WCT-CNT)
                    MOVE AVL-CAB-CABIN-STATUS TO
                         WCT-STATUS-CODE (WCT-CNT)  
                    MOVE SUB            TO DPP02-SIGNED 
                    MOVE DPP02-UNSIGNED TO WCT-OCCUPANCY (WCT-CNT)  
                    MOVE AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY TO
                         WCT-MAX-OCCUPANCY (WCT-CNT)
                    MOVE AVL-CAB-RATE-CODE TO WCT-RATE-CODE (WCT-CNT)   
                 END-IF 
              END-PERFORM.  

           IF AVL-CAB-VIEW-IND <= ' '   
              MOVE '~' TO AVL-CAB-VIEW-IND. 

           PERFORM VARYING WCAT-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
              UNTIL WCAT-SUB > WCAT-CNT 
                 OR (WCAT-RATE-CODE (WCAT-SUB) = AVL-CAB-RATE-CODE  
                AND  WCAT-GROUP-BOOK-NUM (WCAT-SUB) = AVL-CAB-BOOK-NUM  
                AND  WCAT-DECK-CODE (WCAT-SUB) = AVL-CAB-DECK   
                AND  WCAT-FWD-MID-AFT (WCAT-SUB) = AVL-CAB-FWD-AFT-IND  
                AND  WCAT-VIEW-CODE (WCAT-SUB) = AVL-CAB-VIEW-IND   
                AND  WCAT-SUBMETA-CODE (WCAT-SUB) = 
                     TDP01-SUBMETA-CODE (WS-CAT-SUB))   
              CONTINUE  
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WCAT-SUB > WCAT-CNT   
              IF WCAT-CNT >= 99 
                 MOVE 80002 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX 
                 GO TO 9300-SEVERE-ERROR-MSG
              END-IF
              ADD 1 TO WCAT-CNT 
              MOVE LOW-VALUES          TO WS-CAT-AVAIL-ENTRY (WCAT-CNT) 
              MOVE WCAT-CNT            TO WCAT-SUB  
              MOVE AVL-CAB-BOOK-NUM    TO WCAT-GROUP-BOOK-NUM (WCAT-CNT)
              MOVE AVL-CAB-RATE-CODE   TO WCAT-RATE-CODE (WCAT-CNT) 
              MOVE AVL-CAB-DECK        TO WCAT-DECK-CODE (WCAT-CNT) 
              MOVE AVL-CAB-FWD-AFT-IND TO WCAT-FWD-MID-AFT (WCAT-CNT)   
              MOVE AVL-CAB-VIEW-IND    TO WCAT-VIEW-CODE (WCAT-CNT) 
              MOVE TDP01-SUBMETA-CODE (WS-CAT-SUB) TO   
                   WCAT-SUBMETA-CODE (WCAT-CNT).

           ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-DBL (WCAT-SUB).  

           IF AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 2  
              ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-TRPL (WCAT-SUB).  

           IF AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 3  
              ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-QUAD (WCAT-SUB).  

           IF AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 4 THEN 
              ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-QUNT (WCAT-SUB).  

           IF AVL-CAB-WHEELCHAIR-IND = 'S' OR 'R' OR 'L'
              EVALUATE AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY 
                 WHEN 2 ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-SSA-AVAIL-DBL (WCAT-SUB)  
                 WHEN 3 ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-SSA-AVAIL-TRPL (WCAT-SUB) 
                 WHEN 4 ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-SSA-AVAIL-QUAD (WCAT-SUB) 
                 WHEN 5 ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-SSA-AVAIL-QUNT (WCAT-SUB) 
              END-EVALUATE  
           ELSE 
              IF AVL-CAB-WHEELCHAIR-IND = 'P'   
                 EVALUATE AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY  
                    WHEN 2 ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-PART-AVAIL-DBL (WCAT-SUB)  
                    WHEN 3 ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-PART-AVAIL-TRPL (WCAT-SUB) 
                    WHEN 4 ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-PART-AVAIL-QUAD (WCAT-SUB) 
                    WHEN 5 ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-PART-AVAIL-QUNT (WCAT-SUB) 
                 END-EVALUATE   
              ELSE  
                 IF AVL-CAB-WHEELCHAIR-IND = 'Y'
                    EVALUATE AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY   
                       WHEN 2 ADD 1 TO  
                              WCAT-ADA-FULL-AVAIL-DBL (WCAT-SUB)
                       WHEN 3 ADD 1 TO  
                              WCAT-ADA-FULL-AVAIL-TRPL (WCAT-SUB)   
                       WHEN 4 ADD 1 TO  
                              WCAT-ADA-FULL-AVAIL-QUAD (WCAT-SUB)   
                       WHEN 5 ADD 1 TO  
                              WCAT-ADA-FULL-AVAIL-QUNT (WCAT-SUB)   
                 END-EVALUATE.  

           IF AVL-CAB-CABIN-STATUS > SPACES AND 
                 (AVL-CAB-CABIN-STATUS NOT = 'B')   
              GO TO 6000-CREATE-SOI-DATA-EXIT.  

           MOVE 'N' TO WS-DBL-IND   

                       WS-TRPL-IND  
                       WS-QUAD-IND  
                       WS-QUNT-IND. 

           IF (WS-SPOILAGE-CODE-NI = WS-NULL-IND) OR
                 AVL-CAB-CABIN-STATUS = 'B' 
              ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-OPEN-DBL (WCAT-SUB)   
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-DBL-IND
              IF AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 2 THEN  
                 ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-OPEN-TRPL (WCAT-SUB)   
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-TRPL-IND
              END-IF
              IF AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 3 THEN  
                 ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-OPEN-QUAD (WCAT-SUB)   
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-QUAD-IND
              END-IF
              IF AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 4 THEN  
                 ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-OPEN-QUNT (WCAT-SUB)   
                 MOVE 'Y' TO WS-QUNT-IND
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              EVALUATE WS-SPOILAGE-CODE 
                 WHEN 'M'   
                    EVALUATE AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY   
                       WHEN 2   
                          ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-OPEN-DBL (WCAT-SUB)   
                          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-DBL-IND
                       WHEN 3   
                          ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-OPEN-TRPL (WCAT-SUB)  
                          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-TRPL-IND   
                       WHEN 4   
                          ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-OPEN-QUAD (WCAT-SUB)  
                          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-QUAD-IND   
                       WHEN 5   
                          ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-OPEN-QUNT (WCAT-SUB)  
                          MOVE 'Y' TO WS-QUNT-IND   
                    END-EVALUATE
                 WHEN 'N'   
                    IF AVL-CAB-NORMAL-CAPACITY = 2  
                       ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-OPEN-DBL (WCAT-SUB)  
                       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-DBL-IND   
                    END-IF  
                    IF AVL-CAB-NORMAL-CAPACITY <= 3 AND 
                          AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY >= 3 
                       ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-OPEN-TRPL (WCAT-SUB) 
                       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-TRPL-IND  
                    END-IF  
                    IF AVL-CAB-NORMAL-CAPACITY <= 4 AND 
                          AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY >= 4 
                       ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-OPEN-QUAD (WCAT-SUB) 
                       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-QUAD-IND  
                    END-IF  
                    IF AVL-CAB-NORMAL-CAPACITY <= 5 AND 
                          AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY >= 5 
                       ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-OPEN-QUNT (WCAT-SUB) 
                       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-QUNT-IND  
                    END-IF  
                 WHEN '1'   
                    IF AVL-CAB-NORMAL-CAPACITY <= 3 
                       ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-OPEN-DBL (WCAT-SUB)  
                       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-DBL-IND   
                    END-IF  
                    IF AVL-CAB-NORMAL-CAPACITY <= 4 AND 
                          AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY >= 3 
                       ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-OPEN-TRPL (WCAT-SUB) 
                       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-TRPL-IND  
                    END-IF  
                    IF AVL-CAB-NORMAL-CAPACITY <= 5 AND 
                          AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY >= 4 
                       ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-OPEN-QUAD (WCAT-SUB) 
                       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-QUAD-IND  
                    END-IF  
                    IF AVL-CAB-NORMAL-CAPACITY <= 5 AND 
                         AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY >= 5  
                       ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-OPEN-QUNT (WCAT-SUB) 
                       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-QUNT-IND  
                    END-IF  
                 WHEN '2'   
                    IF AVL-CAB-NORMAL-CAPACITY <= 4 
                       ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-OPEN-DBL (WCAT-SUB)  
                       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-DBL-IND   
                    END-IF  
                    IF AVL-CAB-NORMAL-CAPACITY <= 5 AND 
                          AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY >= 3 
                       ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-OPEN-TRPL (WCAT-SUB) 
                       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-TRPL-IND  
                    END-IF  
                    IF AVL-CAB-NORMAL-CAPACITY <= 5 AND 
                          AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY >= 4 
                       ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-OPEN-QUAD (WCAT-SUB) 
                       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-QUAD-IND  
                    END-IF  
                    IF AVL-CAB-NORMAL-CAPACITY <= 5 AND 
                         AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY >= 5  
                       ADD 1 TO WCAT-AVAIL-OPEN-QUNT (WCAT-SUB) 
                       MOVE 'Y' TO WS-QUNT-IND  
                    END-IF  
              END-EVALUATE. 

           IF (WS-SPOILAGE-CODE-NI NOT = WS-NULL-IND) AND   
                 WS-SPOILAGE-CODE = 'M' 
              GO TO 6000-CHECK-ADA. 

           IF WS-DBL-IND = 'Y' AND AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 2 
              ADD 1 TO WCAT-LOG-ADJUST-DBL (WCAT-SUB).  

           IF WS-TRPL-IND = 'Y' AND AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 3
              ADD 1 TO WCAT-LOG-ADJUST-TRPL (WCAT-SUB). 

           IF WS-QUAD-IND = 'Y' AND AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 4
              ADD 1 TO WCAT-LOG-ADJUST-QUAD (WCAT-SUB). 

       6000-CHECK-ADA.  
           IF (AVL-CAB-WHEELCHAIR-IND = ' ' OR 'N') OR  
                 AVL-CAB-CABIN-STATUS = 'B' 
              GO TO 6000-CREATE-SOI-DATA-EXIT.  

           IF AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 2  
              ADD 1 TO WCAT-LOG-ADA-ADJUST-DBL (WCAT-SUB).  

           IF AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 3  
              ADD 1 TO WCAT-LOG-ADA-ADJUST-TRPL (WCAT-SUB). 

           IF AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 4  
              ADD 1 TO WCAT-LOG-ADA-ADJUST-QUAD (WCAT-SUB). 

           IF AVL-CAB-WHEELCHAIR-IND = 'S' OR 'R' OR 'L'
              ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-SSA-OPEN-DBL (WCAT-SUB) 
              IF AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 2   
                 ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-SSA-OPEN-TRPL (WCAT-SUB) 
              END-IF
              IF AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 3   
                 ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-SSA-OPEN-QUAD (WCAT-SUB) 
              END-IF
              IF AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 4   
                 ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-SSA-OPEN-QUNT (WCAT-SUB) 
              END-IF
           ELSE 
              IF AVL-CAB-WHEELCHAIR-IND = 'P'   
                 ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-PART-OPEN-DBL (WCAT-SUB) 
                 IF AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 2
                    ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-PART-OPEN-TRPL (WCAT-SUB) 
                 END-IF 
                 IF AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 3
                    ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-PART-OPEN-QUAD (WCAT-SUB) 
                 END-IF 
                 IF AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 4
                    ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-PART-OPEN-QUNT (WCAT-SUB) 
                 END-IF 
              ELSE  
                 IF AVL-CAB-WHEELCHAIR-IND = 'Y'
                    ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-FULL-OPEN-DBL (WCAT-SUB)  
                    IF AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 2 
                       ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-FULL-OPEN-TRPL (WCAT-SUB)  
                    END-IF  
                    IF AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 3 

                       ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-FULL-OPEN-QUAD (WCAT-SUB)  
                    END-IF  
                    IF AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY > 4 
                       ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-FULL-OPEN-QUNT (WCAT-SUB). 

           IF (WS-SPOILAGE-CODE-NI NOT = WS-NULL-IND)   
              EVALUATE WS-SPOILAGE-CODE 
                 WHEN 'M'   
                    EVALUATE AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY   
                       WHEN 3   
                          ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-DBL (WCAT-SUB)   
                       WHEN 4   
                          ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-DBL (WCAT-SUB)   
                                   WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-TRPL (WCAT-SUB)  
                       WHEN 5   
                          ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-DBL (WCAT-SUB)   
                                   WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-TRPL (WCAT-SUB)  
                                   WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-QUAD (WCAT-SUB)  
                    END-EVALUATE
                 WHEN 'N'   
                    EVALUATE AVL-CAB-NORMAL-CAPACITY
                       WHEN 3   
                          ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-DBL (WCAT-SUB)   
                       WHEN 4   
                          ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-DBL (WCAT-SUB)   
                                   WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-TRPL (WCAT-SUB)  
                       WHEN 5   
                          ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-DBL (WCAT-SUB)   
                                   WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-TRPL (WCAT-SUB)  
                                   WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-QUAD (WCAT-SUB)  
                    END-EVALUATE
                 WHEN '1'   
                    EVALUATE (AVL-CAB-NORMAL-CAPACITY - 1)  
                       WHEN 3   
                          ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-DBL (WCAT-SUB)   
                       WHEN 4   
                          ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-DBL (WCAT-SUB)   
                                   WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-TRPL (WCAT-SUB)  
                       WHEN 5   
                          ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-DBL (WCAT-SUB)   
                                   WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-TRPL (WCAT-SUB)  
                                   WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-QUAD (WCAT-SUB)  
                    END-EVALUATE
                 WHEN '2'   
                    EVALUATE (AVL-CAB-NORMAL-CAPACITY - 2)  
                       WHEN 3   
                          ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-DBL (WCAT-SUB)   
                       WHEN 4   
                          ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-DBL (WCAT-SUB)   
                                   WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-TRPL (WCAT-SUB)  
                       WHEN 5   
                          ADD 1 TO WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-DBL (WCAT-SUB)   
                                   WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-TRPL (WCAT-SUB)  
                                   WCAT-ADA-OFFSET-QUAD (WCAT-SUB)  
                    END-EVALUATE
              END-EVALUATE. 

       6000-CREATE-SOI-DATA-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       8010-PROCESS-CONNECT-CABIN.  
           EXEC SQL DECLARE OLD_CONNECT_CABIN_CUR CURSOR
              SELECT OCCUPANCY,CABIN_NUM,CONNECT_CABIN_NUM, 
                     CATEGORY_CODE,STATUS_CODE,IFNULL(RATE_CODE,' '),   
                     CONNECT_CABIN_CAT_CODE,CONNECT_MAX_OCCUPANCY,  
                     CONNECT_STATUS_CODE
                 FROM SAIL_CONNECT_CABIN
                 WHERE SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE AND 
                       SAIL_ID = :TDP01-SAIL-ID AND 
                       CATEGORY_CODE = :TDP01-CATEGORY-CODE (WS-CAT-SUB)
           END-EXEC.

           EXEC SQL OPEN OLD_CONNECT_CABIN_CUR END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 80010 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           EXEC SQL FETCH NEXT 300 OLD_CONNECT_CABIN_CUR
              INTO WOCT-OCCUPANCY,  
                   WOCT-CABIN-NUM,  
                   WOCT-CONNECT-CABIN-NUM,  
                   WOCT-CATEGORY-CODE,  
                   WOCT-STATUS-CODE,
                   WOCT-RATE-CODE,  
                   WOCT-CONNECT-CABIN-CAT-CODE, 
                   WOCT-CONNECT-MAX-OCCUPANCY,  
                   WOCT-CONNECT-STATUS-CODE 
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 80011 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           IF AUX-INFO-REC-CNT = 300
              MOVE 80012 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           MOVE AUX-INFO-REC-CNT TO WOCT-CNT.   

           EXEC SQL CLOSE OLD_CONNECT_CABIN_CUR END-EXEC.   

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 80013 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           MOVE ALL 'N' TO WS-PROCESS-CNCT-INDS.
           MOVE ZERO    TO WCT-SUB. 

       8011-NEXT-CONNECT-CABIN. 
           ADD 1 TO WCT-SUB.

           IF WCT-SUB > WCT-CNT 
              GO TO 8012-DELETE-UNPROCESSED.

           PERFORM VARYING CNCT-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
                   UNTIL CNCT-SUB > WCT-CNT OR  
                         WCT-CONNECT-CABIN-NUM (WCT-SUB) =  
                         WCT-CABIN-NUM (CNCT-SUB)   
              CONTINUE  
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF CNCT-SUB > WCT-CNT
              MOVE WCT-CONNECT-CABIN-NUM (WCT-SUB) TO SCAR-CABIN-NUM
                                                      AVL-CAB-CABIN-NUM 
              MOVE TDP01-AVAIL-AREA-NAME    TO AVAIL-AREANAME   
              MOVE TDP01-CABIN-AREA-NAME    TO SAILCABIN-CALCAREA   
              MOVE TDP01-SHIP-CODE          TO SCAR-SHIP-CODE   
              MOVE TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD TO SCAR-SAIL-DATE   
              MOVE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS      TO SCAR-DURATION-DAYS   
              FETCH AVAIL-CABIN-REC VIA SAIL-CABIN-AVAIL-SET
                    USING AVL-CAB-CABIN-NUM 
                    ON ERROR GO TO 8018-CONNECT-CABIN-ERROR 
           ELSE 
              MOVE WCT-STATUS-CODE (CNCT-SUB) TO AVL-CAB-CABIN-STATUS   
              MOVE WCT-MAX-OCCUPANCY (CNCT-SUB) TO AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY 
              MOVE WCT-RATE-CODE (CNCT-SUB) TO AVL-CAB-RATE-CODE.   

           PERFORM VARYING WOCT-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
                   UNTIL WOCT-SUB > WOCT-CNT OR 
                         (WOCT-CABIN-NUM (WOCT-SUB) =   
                          WCT-CABIN-NUM (WCT-SUB) AND   
                          WOCT-OCCUPANCY (WOCT-SUB) =   
                          WCT-OCCUPANCY (WCT-SUB))  
              CONTINUE  
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WOCT-SUB > WOCT-CNT   
              GO TO 8011-INSERT-CONNECT-CABIN.  

           MOVE 'Y' TO WS-PROCESS-CNCT-IND (WOCT-SUB).  

           MOVE 0 TO WS-SCC-MOD-SW. 

           IF WOCT-CONNECT-CABIN-NUM (WOCT-SUB) NOT =   
                                       WCT-CONNECT-CABIN-NUM (WCT-SUB)  
              MOVE 1 TO WS-SCC-MOD-SW   
              DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '   
                      TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ' CONNECT CABIN : '  
                      WOCT-CABIN-NUM (WOCT-SUB) ' ' 
                      WOCT-OCCUPANCY (WOCT-SUB) ' ' 
                      'NEW CONNECT CABIN = '
                      WCT-CONNECT-CABIN-NUM (WCT-SUB)   
                      '  OLD VALUE = ' WOCT-CONNECT-CABIN-NUM (WOCT-SUB)
                                                    UPON PRINTER
           END-IF.  

           IF WOCT-CATEGORY-CODE (WOCT-SUB) NOT =   
                                       WCT-CATEGORY-CODE (WCT-SUB)  
              MOVE 1 TO WS-SCC-MOD-SW   
              DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '   
                      TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ' CONNECT CABIN : '  
                      WOCT-CABIN-NUM (WOCT-SUB) ' ' 
                      WOCT-OCCUPANCY (WOCT-SUB) ' ' 
                      'NEW CATEGORY = ' 
                      WCT-CATEGORY-CODE (WCT-SUB)   
                      '  OLD VALUE = ' WOCT-CATEGORY-CODE (WOCT-SUB)
                                                    UPON PRINTER
           END-IF.  

           IF WOCT-STATUS-CODE (WOCT-SUB) NOT = 
                                       WCT-STATUS-CODE (WCT-SUB)
              MOVE 1 TO WS-SCC-MOD-SW   
              DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '   
                      TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ' CONNECT CABIN : '  
                      WOCT-CABIN-NUM (WOCT-SUB) ' ' 
                      WOCT-OCCUPANCY (WOCT-SUB) ' ' 
                      'NEW STATUS CODE = '  
                      WCT-STATUS-CODE (WCT-SUB) 
                      '  OLD VALUE = ' WOCT-STATUS-CODE (WOCT-SUB)  
                                                    UPON PRINTER
           END-IF.  

           IF WOCT-RATE-CODE (WOCT-SUB) NOT = WCT-RATE-CODE (WCT-SUB)   
              MOVE 1 TO WS-SCC-MOD-SW   
              DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '   
                      TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ' CONNECT CABIN : '  
                      WOCT-CABIN-NUM (WOCT-SUB) ' ' 
                      WOCT-OCCUPANCY (WOCT-SUB) ' ' 
                      'NEW RATE CODE = '
                      WCT-RATE-CODE (WCT-SUB)   
                      '  OLD VALUE = ' WOCT-RATE-CODE (WOCT-SUB)
                                                    UPON PRINTER
           END-IF.  

           IF WOCT-CONNECT-CABIN-CAT-CODE (WOCT-SUB)
                                         NOT = AVL-CAB-CATEGORY-CODE
              MOVE 1 TO WS-SCC-MOD-SW   
              DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '   
                      TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ' CONNECT CABIN : '  
                      WOCT-CABIN-NUM (WOCT-SUB) ' ' 
                      WOCT-OCCUPANCY (WOCT-SUB) ' ' 
                      'NEW CONNECT CABIN CAT CODE = '   
                      AVL-CAB-CATEGORY-CODE 
                      '  OLD VALUE = '  
                      WOCT-CONNECT-CABIN-CAT-CODE (WOCT-SUB)
                                                    UPON PRINTER
           END-IF.  

           IF WOCT-CONNECT-MAX-OCCUPANCY (WOCT-SUB) 
                                         NOT = AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY 
              MOVE 1 TO WS-SCC-MOD-SW   
              DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '   
                      TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ' CONNECT CABIN : '  
                      WOCT-CABIN-NUM (WOCT-SUB) ' ' 
                      WOCT-OCCUPANCY (WOCT-SUB) ' ' 
                      'NEW CONNECT CABIN MAX OCC = '
                      AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY  
                      '  OLD VALUE = '  
                      WOCT-CONNECT-MAX-OCCUPANCY (WOCT-SUB) 
                                                    UPON PRINTER
           END-IF.  

           IF WOCT-CONNECT-STATUS-CODE (WOCT-SUB)   
                                         NOT = AVL-CAB-CABIN-STATUS 
              MOVE 1 TO WS-SCC-MOD-SW   
              DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '   
                      TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ' CONNECT CABIN : '  
                      WOCT-CABIN-NUM (WOCT-SUB) ' ' 
                      WOCT-OCCUPANCY (WOCT-SUB) ' ' 
                      'NEW CONNECT CABIN STATUS = ' 
                      AVL-CAB-CABIN-STATUS  
                      '  OLD VALUE = '  
                      WOCT-CONNECT-STATUS-CODE (WOCT-SUB)   
                                                    UPON PRINTER
           END-IF.  

           IF PARAM-TEST-IND = 'T' OR WS-SCC-MOD-SW NOT = 1 
              GO TO 8011-NEXT-CONNECT-CABIN.

           EXEC SQL UPDATE SAIL_CONNECT_CABIN   
                SET CONNECT_CABIN_NUM = 
                                    :WCT-CONNECT-CABIN-NUM (WCT-SUB),   
                    CATEGORY_CODE = :WCT-CATEGORY-CODE (WCT-SUB),   
                    STATUS_CODE = :WCT-STATUS-CODE (WCT-SUB),   
                    RATE_CODE = NULLIF(:WCT-RATE-CODE (WCT-SUB),' '),   
                    CONNECT_CABIN_CAT_CODE = :AVL-CAB-CATEGORY-CODE,
                    CONNECT_MAX_OCCUPANCY = :AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY,  
                    CONNECT_STATUS_CODE = :AVL-CAB-CABIN-STATUS 
              WHERE SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE
                AND SAIL_ID   = :TDP01-SAIL-ID  
                AND OCCUPANCY = :WCT-OCCUPANCY (WCT-SUB)
                AND CABIN_NUM = :WCT-CABIN-NUM (WCT-SUB)
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 80017 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           IF AUX-INFO-PAGE-CNT > 0 
              PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT.  

           GO TO 8011-NEXT-CONNECT-CABIN.   

       8011-INSERT-CONNECT-CABIN.   

           DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '  
                   TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD 
                   ' NEW CONNECT CABIN ENTRY : '
                   WCT-CABIN-NUM (WCT-SUB) ' '  
                   WCT-OCCUPANCY (WCT-SUB) UPON PRINTER.

           IF PARAM-TEST-IND = 'T'  
              GO TO 8011-NEXT-CONNECT-CABIN.

           EXEC SQL INSERT SAIL_CONNECT_CABIN   
              COLUMNS (SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE,
                       SAIL_ID = :TDP01-SAIL-ID,
                       CABIN_NUM = :WCT-CABIN-NUM (WCT-SUB),
                       CONNECT_CABIN_NUM =  
                                      :WCT-CONNECT-CABIN-NUM (WCT-SUB), 
                       CATEGORY_CODE = :WCT-CATEGORY-CODE (WCT-SUB),
                       OCCUPANCY = :WCT-OCCUPANCY (WCT-SUB),
                       STATUS_CODE = :WCT-STATUS-CODE (WCT-SUB),
                       RATE_CODE = NULLIF(:WCT-RATE-CODE (WCT-SUB),' '),
                       CONNECT_CABIN_CAT_CODE = :AVL-CAB-CATEGORY-CODE, 
                       CONNECT_MAX_OCCUPANCY = :AVL-CAB-MAX-CAPACITY,   
                       CONNECT_STATUS_CODE = :AVL-CAB-CABIN-STATUS) 
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 80014 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           IF AUX-INFO-PAGE-CNT > 0 
              PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT.  

           GO TO 8011-NEXT-CONNECT-CABIN.   

       8012-DELETE-UNPROCESSED. 
           PERFORM VARYING WOCT-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
                   UNTIL WOCT-SUB > WOCT-CNT
              IF WS-PROCESS-CNCT-IND (WOCT-SUB) = 'N'   
                 DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '
                         TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD   
                         ' OLD CONNECT CABIN ENTRY DELETED: '   
                         WOCT-CABIN-NUM (WOCT-SUB) ' '  
                         WOCT-OCCUPANCY (WOCT-SUB) UPON PRINTER 
                 IF PARAM-TEST-IND NOT = 'T'
                    EXEC SQL DELETE SAIL_CONNECT_CABIN  
                       WHERE SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE AND   
                             SAIL_ID = :TDP01-SAIL-ID AND   
                             OCCUPANCY = :WOCT-OCCUPANCY (WOCT-SUB) AND 
                             CABIN_NUM = :WOCT-CABIN-NUM (WOCT-SUB) 
                    END-EXEC
                    IF SQLCODE < 0  
                       MOVE 80015 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX   
                       GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR
                    END-IF  
                 END-IF 
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           GO TO 8019-EXIT. 

       8018-CONNECT-CABIN-ERROR.
           MOVE 80016 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX.  

           GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR.   

       8019-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       8040-UPDATE-OPEN-INVENTORY.  
           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL SUB > WS-AVL-INV-CNT OR
                         (WS-AVL-RATE-CODE (SUB) =  
                          WCAT-RATE-CODE (WCAT-SUB) AND 
                          WS-AVL-GROUP-BOOK-NUM (SUB) = 
                          WCAT-GROUP-BOOK-NUM (WCAT-SUB) AND
                          WS-AVL-OCCUPANCY (SUB) = WS-OCCUPANCY AND 
                          WS-AVL-DECK-CODE (SUB) =  
                          WCAT-DECK-CODE (WCAT-SUB) AND 
                          WS-AVL-FWD-MID-AFT (SUB) =
                          WCAT-FWD-MID-AFT (WCAT-SUB) AND   
                          WS-AVL-VIEW-CODE (SUB) =  
                          WCAT-VIEW-CODE (WCAT-SUB) AND 
                          WS-AVL-SUBMETA-CODE (SUB) =   
                          WCAT-SUBMETA-CODE (WCAT-SUB)) 
              CONTINUE  
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF SUB > WS-AVL-INV-CNT  
              GO TO 8040-INSERT-OPEN-INVENTORY. 

           MOVE 0 TO WS-SOP-MOD-SW. 

           IF WS-AVL-OPEN-CABINS (SUB) NOT = WS-OPEN-CABINS 
              MOVE 1 TO WS-SOP-MOD-SW   
              DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '   
                      TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD ' OPEN INVENTORY : ' 
                      TDP01-CATEGORY-CODE (WS-CAT-SUB) ' '  
                      WS-AVL-RATE-CODE (SUB) ' '
                      WS-AVL-GROUP-BOOK-NUM (SUB) ' '   
                      WS-AVL-OCCUPANCY (SUB) ' '
                      WS-AVL-DECK-CODE (SUB) ' '
                      WS-AVL-FWD-MID-AFT (SUB) ' '  
                      WS-AVL-VIEW-CODE (SUB) ' '
                      WS-AVL-SUBMETA-CODE (SUB) ' ' 
                      'NEW OPEN CABINS = ' WS-OPEN-CABINS   
                      '  OLD COUNT = ' WS-AVL-OPEN-CABINS (SUB) 
                                                    UPON PRINTER
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-AVL-OPEN-LOGICAL-ADJUST (SUB) NOT = WS-LOG-ADJUST

              DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '

                      WS-AVL-OCCUPANCY (SUB) ' '
                      WS-AVL-DECK-CODE (SUB) ' '
                      WS-AVL-FWD-MID-AFT (SUB) ' '  
                      WS-AVL-VIEW-CODE (SUB) ' '
                      WS-AVL-SUBMETA-CODE (SUB) ' '

                      '  OLD COUNT = ' WS-AVL-OPEN-LOGICAL-ADJUST (SUB)

           END-IF.
