           EXEC SQL UPDATE SAIL_OPEN_INVENTORY  
                SET OPEN_CABINS = :WS-OPEN-CABINS,  
                    OPEN_LOGICAL_ADJUST = :WS-LOG-ADJUST,   
                    OPEN_SPOILAGE_ADA_ADJUST = :WS-ADA-OFFSET-CABINS,   
                    OPEN_LOGICAL_ADA_ADJUST  = :WS-LOG-ADA-ADJUST,  
                    OPEN_FULL_ADA_CABINS = :WS-ADA-FULL-OPEN-CABINS,
                    OPEN_SSA_ADA_CABINS = :WS-ADA-SSA-OPEN-CABINS,  
                    OPEN_PART_ADA_CABINS = :WS-ADA-PART-OPEN-CABINS,
                    FULL_ADA_BUILT_CABINS = :WS-ADA-FULL-AVAIL-CABINS,  
                    SSA_ADA_BUILT_CABINS = :WS-ADA-SSA-AVAIL-CABINS,
                    PART_ADA_BUILT_CABINS = :WS-ADA-PART-AVAIL-CABINS,  
                    AVAIL_NET_CABINS = :TDP01-EFF-AVAIL-NET-CABINS, 
                    AVAIL_NET_GUESTS = :TDP01-EFF-AVAIL-NET-GUESTS, 
                    AVAIL_GTEE_CABINS = 
                                   NULLIF(:WS-AVAIL-GTEE-CABINS,-9999), 
                    ALLOW_GTEE_CABIN_IND =  
                              :TDP01-ALLOW-GTEE-CABIN-IND (WS-CAT-SUB), 
                    ALLOW_UPG_INTO_IND =
                                :TDP01-ALLOW-UPG-INTO-IND (WS-CAT-SUB), 
                    SAIL_CAT_OCC_INDS = :WS-SC-OCC-INDS,
                    META_CODE = :TDP01-META-CODE (WS-CAT-SUB),  
                    SORT_KEY  = :TDP01-SORT-KEY (WS-CAT-SUB),   
                    ACCESS_CODE = :TDP01-ACCESS-CODE,   
                    GRP_ACCESS_CODE = :TDP01-GROUP-ACCESS-CODE, 
                    ALLOW_CONNECTION_IND = :TDP01-ALLOW-CONNECTION-IND, 
                    ALLOW_GRP_CONNECTION_IND =  
                                       :TDP01-ALLOW-GRP-CONNECTION-IND, 
                    LAST_UPDATE_DATETIME = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(0) 
                WHERE SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE  
                  AND SAIL_ID = :TDP01-SAIL-ID  
                  AND CATEGORY_CODE =   
                                   :TDP01-CATEGORY-CODE (WS-CAT-SUB)
                  AND OCCUPANCY = :WS-OCCUPANCY 
                  AND RATE_CODE = :WCAT-RATE-CODE (WCAT-SUB)
                  AND GROUP_BOOK_NUM =  
                      :WCAT-GROUP-BOOK-NUM (WCAT-SUB)   
                  AND DECK_CODE = :WCAT-DECK-CODE (WCAT-SUB)
                  AND FWD_MID_AFT = :WCAT-FWD-MID-AFT (WCAT-SUB)
                  AND VIEW_CODE = :WCAT-VIEW-CODE (WCAT-SUB)
                  AND SUBMETA_CODE = :WCAT-SUBMETA-CODE (WCAT-SUB)  
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 80020 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

           IF AUX-INFO-REC-CNT > 0  
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-INVENTORY-UPD-IND. 

           IF AUX-INFO-PAGE-CNT > 0 
              PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT.  

           GO TO 8049-EXIT. 

       8040-INSERT-OPEN-INVENTORY.  

           DISPLAY TDP01-SHIP-CODE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' '  
                   TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD 
                   ' NEW OPEN INVENTORY ENTRY : '   
                   TDP01-CATEGORY-CODE (WS-CAT-SUB) ' ' 
                   WCAT-RATE-CODE (WCAT-SUB) ' '
                   WCAT-GROUP-BOOK-NUM (WCAT-SUB) ' '   
                   WS-OCCUPANCY ' ' 
                   WCAT-DECK-CODE (WCAT-SUB) ' '
                   WCAT-FWD-MID-AFT (WCAT-SUB) ' '  
                   WCAT-VIEW-CODE (WCAT-SUB) ' '
                   WCAT-SUBMETA-CODE (WCAT-SUB) ' ' UPON PRINTER.   

           IF PARAM-TEST-IND = 'T'  
              GO TO 8049-EXIT.  


           IF WS-GROUP-BOOK-NUM-NI = WS-NULL-IND
              MOVE SPACES TO WCAT-GROUP-BOOK-NUM (WCAT-SUB) 
           END-IF.  

           EXEC SQL INSERT SAIL_OPEN_INVENTORY  
                (SHIP_CODE,SAIL_ID,CATEGORY_CODE,OCCUPANCY, 
                 RATE_CODE,GROUP_BOOK_NUM,DECK_CODE,FWD_MID_AFT,
                 VIEW_CODE,SUBMETA_CODE,OPEN_CABINS,
                 AVAIL_NET_CABINS,AVAIL_NET_GUESTS,AVAIL_GTEE_CABINS,   
                 ALLOW_GTEE_CABIN_IND,ALLOW_UPG_INTO_IND,META_CODE, 
                 OPEN_SPOILAGE_ADA_ADJUST,OPEN_FULL_ADA_CABINS, 
                 OPEN_SSA_ADA_CABINS,OPEN_PART_ADA_CABINS,  
                 FULL_ADA_BUILT_CABINS,SSA_ADA_BUILT_CABINS,
                 PART_ADA_BUILT_CABINS,SORT_KEY,SAIL_CAT_OCC_INDS,  
                 OPEN_LOGICAL_ADJUST,OPEN_LOGICAL_ADA_ADJUST,   
                 RECAT_CODE,ACCESS_CODE,GRP_ACCESS_CODE,
                 ALLOW_CONNECTION_IND,ALLOW_GRP_CONNECTION_IND, 
PRCCMB           LAST_UPDATE_DATETIME,PHANTOM_CAT_IND)  
              VALUES
                (:TDP01-SHIP-CODE,:TDP01-SAIL-ID,   
                 :TDP01-CATEGORY-CODE (WS-CAT-SUB),:WS-OCCUPANCY,   
                 :WCAT-RATE-CODE (WCAT-SUB),
                 :WCAT-GROUP-BOOK-NUM (WCAT-SUB),   
                 :WCAT-DECK-CODE (WCAT-SUB),
                 :WCAT-FWD-MID-AFT (WCAT-SUB),  
                 :WCAT-VIEW-CODE (WCAT-SUB),
                 :WCAT-SUBMETA-CODE (WCAT-SUB), 
                 :WS-OPEN-CABINS,:TDP01-EFF-AVAIL-NET-CABINS,   
                 :TDP01-EFF-AVAIL-NET-GUESTS,   
                 NULLIF(:WS-AVAIL-GTEE-CABINS,-9999),   
                 :TDP01-ALLOW-GTEE-CABIN-IND (WS-CAT-SUB),  
                 :TDP01-ALLOW-UPG-INTO-IND (WS-CAT-SUB),
                 :TDP01-META-CODE (WS-CAT-SUB),:WS-ADA-OFFSET-CABINS,   
                 :WS-ADA-FULL-OPEN-CABINS,:WS-ADA-SSA-OPEN-CABINS,  
                 :WS-ADA-PART-OPEN-CABINS,:WS-ADA-FULL-AVAIL-CABINS,
                 :WS-ADA-SSA-AVAIL-CABINS,:WS-ADA-PART-AVAIL-CABINS,
                 :TDP01-SORT-KEY (WS-CAT-SUB),:WS-SC-OCC-INDS,  
                 :WS-LOG-ADJUST,:WS-LOG-ADA-ADJUST,'N', 
                 :TDP01-ACCESS-CODE,:TDP01-GROUP-ACCESS-CODE,   
                 :TDP01-ALLOW-CONNECTION-IND,   
                 :TDP01-ALLOW-GRP-CONNECTION-IND,   
PRCCMB           CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(0),  
PRCCMB           :TDP01-PHANTOM-CAT-IND (WS-CAT-SUB))   
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 80019 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           IF AUX-INFO-REC-CNT > 0  
              MOVE 'Y' TO WS-INVENTORY-UPD-IND. 

           IF AUX-INFO-PAGE-CNT > 0 
              PERFORM DPP12-COMMIT THRU DPP12-COMMIT-EXIT.  

       8049-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       8000-WRITE-PRINT-REC.

           IF WS-LINE-CNT < 53  
              GO TO 8050-PRINT-DETAIL.  

           ADD  1            TO WS-PAGE-CNT.

           MOVE WS-PAGE-CNT  TO HDG1-PAGE   
           MOVE 5            TO WS-LINE-CNT 

           WRITE PRINT-REC FROM HDG1 AFTER PAGE.

           WRITE PRINT-REC FROM HDG2 AFTER 1.   

           WRITE PRINT-REC FROM ASTERISK-LINE AFTER 1.  

           WRITE PRINT-REC FROM HDG4 AFTER 1.   

           WRITE PRINT-REC FROM ASTERISK-LINE AFTER 1.  

       8050-PRINT-DETAIL.   

           MOVE SSP20-O-SHIP-NAME          TO WS-DET-SHIP.  
           MOVE TDP01-DURATION-DAYS        TO WS-DET-DURATION.  
           MOVE TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD   TO DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DATE.  
           MOVE DPP02-YYYYMMDD-MM          TO WS-DET-SAIL-MM
           MOVE DPP02-YYYYMMDD-DD          TO WS-DET-SAIL-DD
           MOVE DPP02-YYYYMMDD-YYYY        TO WS-DET-SAIL-YYYY. 
           MOVE AVAIL-CATEGORY-CODE        TO WS-DET-CATEGORY-CODE. 
           MOVE AVL-CAB-CABIN-NUM          TO WS-DET-CABIN-NUM. 

           WRITE PRINT-REC FROM WS-DETAIL-LINE AFTER 1. 

           MOVE SPACES TO WS-DET-USERID 
                          WS-DET-BOOK1  
                          WS-DET-BOOK2. 

           ADD 1      TO WS-LINE-CNT.   

       8099-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       6400-LIST-ALL-RATE-FOLLOWER. 
           EXEC SQL DECLARE RATE_FOLLOWER_CUR CURSOR FOR RETENTION  
               SELECT PARENT_CODE,  
                      CHILD_RATE_CODE   
                 FROM SAIL_RATE_LEADER  
                 WHERE SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE 
                   AND SAIL_ID = :TDP01-SAIL-ID 
                   AND CONTROL_TYPE = 'CABIN'   
           END-EXEC.

           EXEC SQL OPEN RATE_FOLLOWER_CUR END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-RATE-LDR-CNT.  

       6501-NEXT-RATE-FOLLOWER. 
           EXEC SQL FETCH NEXT 510 RATE_FOLLOWER_CUR
                    INTO :WS-TMP-LDR-PARENT-CODE
                        ,:WS-TMP-CHILD-RATE-CODE
           END-EXEC 

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                     UNTIL SUB > AUX-INFO-REC-CNT   
              IF WS-TMP-LDR-PARENT-CODE (SUB) (4:1) NOT > SPACES
                 ADD 1             TO WS-RATE-LDR-CNT   
                 MOVE WS-RATE-LDR-CNT   
                                   TO WS-SUB
                 MOVE WS-TMP-LDR-PARENT-CODE (SUB)  
                                   TO WS-LDR-PARENT-CODE (WS-SUB)   
                 MOVE WS-TMP-CHILD-RATE-CODE (SUB)  
                                   TO WS-LDR-CHILD-RATE-CODE (WS-SUB)   
166670*          MOVE SPACES       TO WS-LDR-CABIN-LINK-IND (WS-SUB)
166670           MOVE SPACES       TO WS-CHLD-CABIN-LINK-IND (WS-SUB)   
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF AUX-INFO-REC-CNT >= 510   
              GO TO 6501-NEXT-RATE-FOLLOWER.

           EXEC SQL CLOSE RATE_FOLLOWER_CUR END-EXEC

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

       6499-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       6500-FIND-RTE-FAM-SOLD-CNT.  
           MOVE ZEROES       TO FLW-CNT 
                                WS-CALC-FAMILY-SOLD-CNT.

           MOVE WS-RATE-CODE TO WS-LDR-RATE-CODE.   
           PERFORM 6600-ADD-FOLLOWER-RATE-CODE THRU 6699-EXIT   
                   VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                     UNTIL SUB > WS-RATE-LDR-CNT.   

           PERFORM VARYING WS-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 UNTIL WS-SUB > FLW-CNT
              MOVE WS-FLW-RATE-CODE (WS-SUB) TO WS-LDR-RATE-CODE
              PERFORM 6600-ADD-FOLLOWER-RATE-CODE THRU 6699-EXIT
                      VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                        UNTIL SUB > WS-RATE-LDR-CNT 
           END-PERFORM. 

       6501-CALC-FAMILY-CNT.
           MOVE 0 TO WS-SUB.

       6502-NEXT-FOLLOWER-RATE. 
           ADD 1  TO WS-SUB.

           IF WS-SUB > FLW-CNT  
              GO TO 6503-POPULATE-SR-TABLE. 

           PERFORM VARYING WS-SR-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
                     UNTIL WS-SR-SUB > WS-SR-CNT
                        OR WS-SR-RATE-CODE (WS-SR-SUB) =
                           WS-FLW-RATE-CODE (WS-SUB)
              CONTINUE  
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SR-SUB > WS-SR-CNT 
              GO TO 6502-NEXT-FOLLOWER-RATE.

           ADD WS-SR-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB) TO WS-CALC-FAMILY-SOLD-CNT.  

           PERFORM VARYING WS-SR-CAT-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
                     UNTIL WS-SR-CAT-SUB >  
                           WS-SR-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SR-SUB)  
              PERFORM VARYING CAT-SUB FROM 1 BY 1   
                        UNTIL CAT-SUB > CAT-CNT 
                           OR WS-FAM-CAT-CODE (CAT-SUB) =   
                              WS-SR-CAT-CODE (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB)  
                 CONTINUE   
              END-PERFORM   
              IF CAT-SUB > CAT-CNT  
                 ADD 1 TO CAT-CNT   
                 MOVE WS-SR-CAT-CODE (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB) TO   
                      WS-FAM-CAT-CODE (CAT-CNT) 
                 MOVE WS-SR-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB) TO  
                      WS-FAM-CAT-CABIN-CNT (CAT-CNT)
              ELSE  
                 ADD  WS-SR-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB) TO  
                      WS-FAM-CAT-CABIN-CNT (CAT-SUB)
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 


           GO TO 6502-NEXT-FOLLOWER-RATE.   

       6503-POPULATE-SR-TABLE.  
           PERFORM VARYING WS-SR-SUB FROM 1 BY 1
                     UNTIL WS-SR-SUB > WS-SR-CNT
                        OR WS-SR-RATE-CODE (WS-SR-SUB) = WS-RATE-CODE   
              CONTINUE  
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SR-SUB > WS-SR-CNT 
              ADD 1                   TO WS-SR-CNT  
              MOVE WS-SR-CNT          TO WS-SR-SUB  
              MOVE LOW-VALUES         TO WS-SR-ENTRY     (WS-SR-SUB)
              MOVE WS-RATE-CODE       TO
                   WS-SR-RATE-CODE (WS-SR-SUB)  
           END-IF.  

           MOVE WS-CALC-FAMILY-SOLD-CNT TO  
                WS-SR-FAMILY-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB). 

           PERFORM VARYING CAT-SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                     UNTIL CAT-SUB > CAT-CNT
              PERFORM VARYING WS-SR-CAT-SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
                        UNTIL WS-SR-CAT-SUB >   
                              WS-SR-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SR-SUB)   
                           OR WS-FAM-CAT-CODE (CAT-SUB) =   
                              WS-SR-CAT-CODE (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB)  
                 CONTINUE   
              END-PERFORM   
              IF WS-SR-CAT-SUB > WS-SR-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SR-SUB)
                 ADD 1 TO WS-SR-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SR-SUB)   
                 MOVE WS-SR-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SR-SUB) TO
                      WS-SR-CAT-SUB 
                 MOVE ZEROES TO 
                      WS-SR-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB) 
                      WS-SR-CAT-SOLD-REQS (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB) 
                 MOVE WS-FAM-CAT-CODE (CAT-SUB) TO  
                      WS-SR-CAT-CODE (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB)  
                 MOVE WS-FAM-CAT-CABIN-CNT (CAT-SUB) TO 
                      WS-SR-CAT-FAMILY-CABIN-CNT
                                            (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB)   
              ELSE  
                 ADD  WS-FAM-CAT-CABIN-CNT (CAT-SUB) TO 
                      WS-SR-CAT-FAMILY-CABIN-CNT
                                            (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB)   
              END-IF
           END-PERFORM. 

       6599-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       6600-ADD-FOLLOWER-RATE-CODE. 

           IF WS-LDR-RATE-CODE NOT = WS-LDR-PARENT-CODE (SUB)   
              GO TO 6699-EXIT.  

166670*    IF WS-LDR-CABIN-LINK-IND (SUB) > SPACES  
166670     IF WS-CHLD-CABIN-LINK-IND (SUB) > SPACES 
              GO TO 6601-CHECK-CABIN-LINK.  

           EXEC SQL 
              SELECT CABIN_LINK_IND 
166670*         INTO :WS-LDR-CABIN-LINK-IND (SUB)   
166670          INTO :WS-CHLD-CABIN-LINK-IND (SUB)  
                FROM SAIL_RATE  
               WHERE SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE   
                 AND SAIL_ID   = :TDP01-SAIL-ID 
166670*          AND RATE_CODE = :WS-LDR-RATE-CODE  
166670           AND RATE_CODE = :WS-LDR-CHILD-RATE-CODE (SUB)  
              FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           IF SQLCODE = 100 
              GO TO 6699-EXIT.  

       6601-CHECK-CABIN-LINK.   
166670*    IF WS-LDR-CABIN-LINK-IND (SUB) NOT = 'Y' 
166670     IF WS-CHLD-CABIN-LINK-IND (SUB) NOT = 'Y'
166670        GO TO 6699-EXIT.  

           ADD 1 TO FLW-CNT.
           MOVE WS-LDR-CHILD-RATE-CODE (SUB) TO 
                WS-FLW-RATE-CODE (FLW-CNT). 

       6699-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       6800-GET-GIFT-OBC.   
           EXEC SQL DECLARE GIFT_OBC_CUR CURSOR 
              SELECT GIFT_CODE  
                 FROM  ITM_GIFT_DETAIL  
                 WHERE ITEM_TYPE   = 'BV'   
                  AND  ITEM_CODE   = :WS-CHK-GIFT-ITEM-CODE 
                  AND  BUNDLE_CODE = :WS-CHK-GIFT-BUNDLE-CODE   
                  AND  GIFT_TYPE = 'O'  
           END-EXEC.

           EXEC SQL 
                OPEN GIFT_OBC_CUR   
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

       6801-PROCESS-GIFT.   
           EXEC SQL 
               FETCH NEXT GIFT_OBC_CUR  
                 INTO  :WS-GIFT-CODE
           END-EXEC.
           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           ELSE 
              IF SQLCODE = 100  
                 GO TO 6802-CLOSE-GIFT-CURSOR   
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           EXEC SQL 
              SELECT POS_1_OBC_AMT  
                    ,POS_2_OBC_AMT  
                    ,POS_3_OBC_AMT  
                    ,POS_4_OBC_AMT  
                    ,POS_5_OBC_AMT  
               INTO :WS-POS-1-GIFT-OBC-AMT  
                   ,:WS-POS-2-GIFT-OBC-AMT  
                   ,:WS-POS-3-GIFT-OBC-AMT  
                   ,:WS-POS-4-GIFT-OBC-AMT  
                   ,:WS-POS-5-GIFT-OBC-AMT  
               FROM ITM_GIFT_DETAIL_CAT 
              WHERE ITEM_TYPE   = 'BV'  
                AND ITEM_CODE   = :WS-CHK-GIFT-ITEM-CODE
                AND BUNDLE_CODE = :WS-CHK-GIFT-BUNDLE-CODE  
                AND GIFT_TYPE   = 'O'   
                AND GIFT_CODE   = :WS-GIFT-CODE 
                AND META_CODE IN (:WS-CHK-CPA-META-CODE,'~~')   
                AND CATEGORY_CODE IN (:WS-CHK-CPA-CAT-CODE,'~~~')   
              FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           ELSE 
              IF SQLCODE = 100  
                 GO TO 6801-PROCESS-GIFT
              END-IF
           END-IF.  

           ADD WS-POS-1-GIFT-OBC-AMT TO WS-CHK-POS-1-GIFT-OBC-AMT.  
           ADD WS-POS-2-GIFT-OBC-AMT TO WS-CHK-POS-2-GIFT-OBC-AMT.  
           ADD WS-POS-3-GIFT-OBC-AMT TO WS-CHK-POS-3-GIFT-OBC-AMT.  
           ADD WS-POS-4-GIFT-OBC-AMT TO WS-CHK-POS-4-GIFT-OBC-AMT.  
           ADD WS-POS-5-GIFT-OBC-AMT TO WS-CHK-POS-5-GIFT-OBC-AMT.  

           GO TO 6801-PROCESS-GIFT. 

       6802-CLOSE-GIFT-CURSOR.  
           EXEC SQL 
                CLOSE GIFT_OBC_CUR  
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           END-IF.  

       6899-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       6900-CAH-SOLD-CNT.   
           FETCH FIRST CABIN-HOLD-REC WITHIN CABIN-HOLD AREA
                 ON ERROR GO TO 6911-CAH-ERROR. 

           GO TO 6902-PROCESS-CAH.  

       6901-FETCH-NEXT-CAH. 
           FIND CURRENT CABIN-HOLD-REC RECORD.  

           FETCH NEXT CABIN-HOLD-REC WITHIN CABIN-HOLD AREA 
                 AT END GO TO 6999-CAH-SOLD-CNT-EXIT
                 ON ERROR GO TO 6911-CAH-ERROR. 

       6902-PROCESS-CAH.

           IF CAH-SHIP-CODE     NOT = TDP01-SHIP-CODE OR
              CAH-SAIL-DATE     NOT = TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD OR   
              CAH-DURATION-DAYS NOT = TDP01-DURATION-DAYS   
              GO TO 6901-FETCH-NEXT-CAH 
           END-IF.  

       6903-BUILD-CAH-SAIL-OFF-ID.  
           MOVE ZERO TO WS-SOLD-SUB.

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                                    UNTIL SUB > WS-SOLD-CNT 
                IF CAH-OFFER-ID (1) = WS-SOLD-OFFER-ID (SUB)
                   MOVE SUB          TO WS-SOLD-SUB 
                   MOVE WS-SOLD-CNT  TO SUB 
                END-IF  
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SOLD-SUB = ZERO
              ADD 1                  TO WS-SOLD-CNT 
              MOVE WS-SOLD-CNT       TO WS-SOLD-SUB 

              MOVE LOW-VALUES        TO WS-SOLD-ENTRY     (WS-SOLD-SUB) 

              MOVE CAH-OFFER-ID (1) 
                TO WS-SOLD-OFFER-ID   (WS-SOLD-SUB) 

           END-IF.  

           ADD 1                     TO WS-SOLD-CABIN-CNT (WS-SOLD-SUB).
980649     MOVE 'Y'                  TO WS-SOLD-BOOKED-IND(WS-SOLD-SUB).

       6904-BUILD-CAH-SAIL-OFF-PRC. 
           IF CAH-REQ-CPA > SPACES  
              MOVE CAH-REQ-CPA       TO WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE 
           ELSE 
              MOVE CAH-CATEGORY-CODE TO WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE 
           END-IF.  

PRCCMB*    IF CAH-CABIN-NUM (1:1) = 'T' 
PRCCMB        MOVE 'B'               TO WS-CABIN-TYPE.  
PRCCMB*       MOVE 'G'               TO WS-CABIN-TYPE   
PRCCMB*    ELSE 
PRCCMB*       MOVE 'C'               TO WS-CABIN-TYPE   
PRCCMB*    END-IF.  

           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-SOLD-CAT-SUB.  

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL SUB > WS-SOLD-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SOLD-SUB)  

             IF WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE =   
                            WS-SOLD-CPA-CAT-CODE (WS-SOLD-SUB SUB) AND  
                CAH-REQ-CAT-CODE =  
                            WS-SOLD-CAT-CODE (WS-SOLD-SUB SUB)     AND  
                WS-CABIN-TYPE = WS-SOLD-CABIN-TYPE (WS-SOLD-SUB SUB)

                MOVE SUB TO WS-SOLD-CAT-SUB 
                MOVE WS-SOLD-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SOLD-SUB) TO SUB 

             END-IF 

           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SOLD-CAT-SUB = 0   
              ADD 1 TO WS-SOLD-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SOLD-SUB)  

              MOVE WS-SOLD-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SOLD-SUB)  
                TO WS-SOLD-CAT-SUB  

              MOVE WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE  
                TO WS-SOLD-CPA-CAT-CODE (WS-SOLD-SUB WS-SOLD-CAT-SUB)   

              MOVE CAH-CATEGORY-CODE
                TO WS-SOLD-CAT-CODE (WS-SOLD-SUB WS-SOLD-CAT-SUB)   

              MOVE WS-CABIN-TYPE
                TO WS-SOLD-CABIN-TYPE (WS-SOLD-SUB WS-SOLD-CAT-SUB) 

           END-IF.  

           ADD 1 TO WS-SOLD-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-SOLD-SUB WS-SOLD-CAT-SUB).
980649     MOVE 'Y' 
980649           TO WS-SOLD-CAT-BOOKED-IND(WS-SOLD-SUB WS-SOLD-CAT-SUB).

       6905-BUILD-CAH-SAIL-RATE.

           MOVE ZEROES             TO WS-SR-SUB.

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                               UNTIL SUB > WS-SR-CNT

            IF CAH-OFFER-CODE (1:3) = WS-SR-RATE-CODE (SUB) 
               MOVE SUB                     TO WS-SR-SUB
               MOVE WS-SR-CNT               TO SUB  
            END-IF  
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SR-SUB = 0 
              ADD 1                   TO WS-SR-CNT  
              MOVE WS-SR-CNT          TO WS-SR-SUB  

              MOVE LOW-VALUES         TO WS-SR-ENTRY     (WS-SR-SUB)

              MOVE CAH-OFFER-CODE (1:3) 
                TO WS-SR-RATE-CODE (WS-SR-SUB)  
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1 TO WS-SR-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB).

       6906-BUILD-CAH-SAIL-RATE-CAT.

           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-SR-CAT-SUB.

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL SUB > WS-SR-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SR-SUB)  

             IF WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE = WS-SR-CAT-CODE (WS-SR-SUB SUB)
                MOVE SUB                             TO WS-SR-CAT-SUB   
                MOVE WS-SR-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SR-SUB) TO SUB 
             END-IF 

           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SR-CAT-SUB = 0 
              ADD 1           TO WS-SR-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SR-SUB)

              MOVE WS-SR-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SR-SUB) TO WS-SR-CAT-SUB 

              MOVE WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE  
                TO WS-SR-CAT-CODE (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB) 
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1 TO WS-SR-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-SR-SUB WS-SR-CAT-SUB).  

       6907-BUILD-CAH-SAIL-PROMO.   

           IF CAH-OFFER-CODE (4:4) NOT > SPACES 
              GO TO 6920-FETCH-FIRST-CHCR.  

           MOVE ZEROES             TO WS-SP-SUB.

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                               UNTIL SUB > WS-SP-CNT

            IF CAH-OFFER-CODE (4:4) = WS-SP-PROMO-CODE (SUB)
               MOVE SUB                     TO WS-SP-SUB
               MOVE WS-SP-CNT               TO SUB  
            END-IF  
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SP-SUB = 0 
              ADD 1                   TO WS-SP-CNT  
              MOVE WS-SP-CNT          TO WS-SP-SUB  

              MOVE LOW-VALUES         TO WS-SP-ENTRY     (WS-SP-SUB)

              MOVE CAH-OFFER-CODE (4:4) 
                TO WS-SP-PROMO-CODE (WS-SP-SUB) 
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1 TO WS-SP-CABIN-CNT (WS-SP-SUB).

       6908-BUILD-CAH-SAIL-PROMO-CAT.   

           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-SP-CAT-SUB.

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL SUB > WS-SP-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SP-SUB)  

             IF WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE = WS-SP-CAT-CODE (WS-SP-SUB SUB)
                MOVE SUB                             TO WS-SP-CAT-SUB   
                MOVE WS-SP-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SP-SUB) TO SUB 
             END-IF 

           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SP-CAT-SUB = 0 
              ADD 1           TO WS-SP-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SP-SUB)

              MOVE WS-SP-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SP-SUB) TO WS-SP-CAT-SUB 

              MOVE WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE  
                TO WS-SP-CAT-CODE (WS-SP-SUB WS-SP-CAT-SUB) 
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1 TO WS-SP-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-SP-SUB WS-SP-CAT-SUB).  

       6909-BUILD-CAH-SAL-RTE-PRO.  

           MOVE ZEROES             TO WS-SRP-SUB.   

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                               UNTIL SUB > WS-SRP-CNT   

            IF CAH-OFFER-CODE (1:7) = WS-SRP-RATE-PROMO-CODE (SUB)  
               MOVE SUB                     TO WS-SRP-SUB   
               MOVE WS-SRP-CNT              TO SUB  
            END-IF  
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SRP-SUB = 0
              ADD 1                   TO WS-SRP-CNT 
              MOVE WS-SP-CNT          TO WS-SRP-SUB 

              MOVE LOW-VALUES         TO WS-SRP-ENTRY     (WS-SRP-SUB)  

              MOVE CAH-OFFER-CODE (1:7) 
                TO WS-SRP-RATE-PROMO-CODE (WS-SRP-SUB)  
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1 TO WS-SRP-CABIN-CNT (WS-SRP-SUB).  

       6910-BUILD-CAH-SAL-RTE-PRO-CAT.  

           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-SRP-CAT-SUB.   

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL SUB > WS-SRP-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SRP-SUB)

             IF WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE = WS-SRP-CAT-CODE (WS-SRP-SUB SUB)  
                MOVE SUB                               TO WS-SRP-CAT-SUB
                MOVE WS-SRP-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SRP-SUB) TO SUB   
             END-IF 

           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SRP-CAT-SUB = 0
              ADD 1           TO WS-SRP-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SRP-SUB)  

              MOVE WS-SRP-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SRP-SUB) TO WS-SRP-CAT-SUB  

              MOVE WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE  
                TO WS-SRP-CAT-CODE (WS-SRP-SUB WS-SRP-CAT-SUB)  
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1 TO WS-SRP-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-SRP-SUB WS-SRP-CAT-SUB).   

           GO TO 6920-FETCH-FIRST-CHCR. 

       6911-CAH-ERROR.  
           IF ERROR-NUM = '0013' AND '0007' AND '0006'  
              GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR 
           ELSE 
              GO TO 6999-CAH-SOLD-CNT-EXIT  
           END-IF.  

       6920-FETCH-FIRST-CHCR.   

           FETCH FIRST CABIN-HOLD-CONT-REC  
                 WITHIN CABIN-HOLD-CONT-SET SET 
                 ON ERROR GO TO 6925-CHCR-ERROR.

           GO TO 6922-PROCESS-CHCR. 

       6921-FETCH-NEXT-CHCR.
           FETCH NEXT  CABIN-HOLD-CONT-REC  
                 WITHIN CABIN-HOLD-CONT-SET SET 
                 AT END GO TO 6901-FETCH-NEXT-CAH   
                 ON ERROR GO TO 6925-CHCR-ERROR.

       6922-PROCESS-CHCR.   

           IF CHCR-ENTITY-OWNER-CODE NOT = 'ADB'
              GO TO 6921-FETCH-NEXT-CHCR
           END-IF.  

           EXEC SQL 
              SELECT ADD_BENEFIT_CODE   
                    ,ADD_BENEFIT_TYPE   
                INTO :WS-ADD-BNFT-CODE  
                    ,:WS-ADD-BNFT-TYPE  
                FROM ADD_BENEFIT_MASTER 
               WHERE PRP_OWNER_CODE = :CHCR-ENTITY-OWNER-CODE   
                 AND PRP_OWNER_ID   = :CHCR-ENTITY-OWNER-ID 
              FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              DISPLAY 'ERROR FETCHING CHCR:' CHCR-ENTITY-OWNER-CODE 
                  ':' CHCR-ENTITY-OWNER-ID ':' SQLCODE ':'  
                  CAH-CALC-KEY  UPON PRINTER
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
           ELSE 
              IF SQLCODE = 100  
              DISPLAY 'CHCR NOT FOUND:' CHCR-ENTITY-OWNER-CODE  
                  ':' CHCR-ENTITY-OWNER-ID ':' CAH-CALC-KEY UPON PRINTER
                 GO TO 6921-FETCH-NEXT-CHCR 
           END-IF.  

           IF WS-ADD-BNFT-TYPE = 'E'
              GO TO 6921-FETCH-NEXT-CHCR

           END-IF.  

       6923-BUILD-CAH-SAIL-ADB. 

            MOVE ZEROES TO WS-SAB-SUB.  

            PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1 
                                UNTIL SUB > WS-SAB-CNT  

             IF WS-SAB-CODE (SUB) = WS-ADD-BNFT-CODE
                 MOVE SUB             TO WS-SAB-SUB 
                 MOVE WS-SAB-CNT      TO SUB
             END-IF 
           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SAB-SUB = 0
              ADD 1                   TO WS-SAB-CNT 
              MOVE WS-SAB-CNT         TO WS-SAB-SUB 
              MOVE LOW-VALUES         TO WS-SAB-ENTRY     (WS-SAB-SUB)  
              MOVE WS-ADD-BNFT-CODE   TO  WS-SAB-CODE (WS-SAB-SUB)  
           END-IF.  

           ADD 1 TO WS-SAB-CABIN-CNT (WS-SAB-SUB).  

       6924-BUILD-CAH-SAIL-ADB-CAT. 

           MOVE ZEROES TO WS-SAB-CAT-SUB.   

           PERFORM VARYING SUB FROM 1 BY 1  
                   UNTIL SUB > WS-SAB-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SAB-SUB)

             IF WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE =   
                            WS-SAB-CAT-CODE (WS-SAB-SUB SUB)

                MOVE SUB TO WS-SAB-CAT-SUB  
                MOVE  WS-SAB-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SAB-SUB) TO SUB  
             END-IF 

           END-PERFORM. 

           IF WS-SAB-CAT-SUB = 0
              ADD 1           TO WS-SAB-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SAB-SUB)  

              MOVE WS-SAB-CAT-ENTRY-CNT (WS-SAB-SUB)
                TO WS-SAB-CAT-SUB   

              MOVE WS-PROCESS-CAT-CODE  
                TO WS-SAB-CAT-CODE (WS-SAB-SUB WS-SAB-CAT-SUB)  

           END-IF.  

           ADD 1 TO WS-SAB-CAT-CABIN-CNT (WS-SAB-SUB WS-SAB-CAT-SUB).   

           GO TO 6921-FETCH-NEXT-CHCR.  

       6925-CHCR-ERROR. 
           IF ERROR-NUM NOT = '0013' AND '0007' AND '0006'  
              GO TO 9100-DATABASE-ERROR 
           ELSE 
              GO TO 6901-FETCH-NEXT-CAH 
           END-IF.  

       6999-CAH-SOLD-CNT-EXIT.  
           EXIT.

       8200-BALANCE-ADA-PHANTOM.
           EXEC SQL DECLARE PHANTOM_META_CUR CURSOR FOR RETENTION   
              SELECT SC.CATEGORY_CODE,META_CODE,OCCUPANCY   
                    ,SUM(OPEN_FULL_ADA_CABINS)  
                    ,SUM(OPEN_SSA_ADA_CABINS)   
                    ,SUM(OPEN_PART_ADA_CABINS)  
                 FROM SAIL_CATEGORY SC  
                 JOIN SAIL_OPEN_INVENTORY SOI   
                   ON SOI.SHIP_CODE = SC.SHIP_CODE  
                  AND SOI.SAIL_ID = SC.SAIL_ID  
                  AND SOI.META_CODE = SC.META_CODE  
                  AND SOI.CATEGORY_CODE <> SC.CATEGORY_CODE 
                  AND SOI.RATE_CODE = '~'   
                  AND SOI.GROUP_BOOK_NUM = '~'  
                 WHERE SC.SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE  
                   AND SC.SAIL_ID = :TDP01-SAIL-ID  
                   AND SC.PHANTOM_CAT_IND = 'Y' 
                 GROUP BY SC.CATEGORY_CODE,META_CODE,OCCUPANCY  
                 ORDER BY SC.CATEGORY_CODE,META_CODE,OCCUPANCY  
           END-EXEC.

           EXEC SQL OPEN PHANTOM_META_CUR END-EXEC. 

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 82001 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           MOVE SPACES TO WS-LAST-CAT-CODE  
                          WS-LAST-META-CODE.

           EXEC SQL WHENEVER NOT FOUND GO TO 8210-CLOSE-META-CUR
           END-EXEC.

       8205-NEXT-PHANTOM-CAT.   
           EXEC SQL FETCH NEXT PHANTOM_META_CUR 
              INTO WS-PHANTOM-CAT-CODE  
                  ,WS-META-CODE 
                  ,OCC-SUB  
                  ,WS-OPEN-FULL-ADA-CABINS  
                  ,WS-OPEN-SSA-ADA-CABINS   
                  ,WS-OPEN-PART-ADA-CABINS  
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 82002 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           EXEC SQL WHENEVER NOT FOUND CONTINUE END-EXEC.   

           IF (WS-PHANTOM-CAT-CODE NOT = WS-LAST-CAT-CODE)  
              IF WS-LAST-CAT-CODE > SPACES  
                 PERFORM 8250-GET-ADA-UPG-INFO THRU 8299-EXIT   
              END-IF
              MOVE WS-PHANTOM-CAT-CODE TO WS-LAST-CAT-CODE  
              MOVE WS-META-CODE        TO WS-LAST-META-CODE 
              MOVE LOW-VALUES          TO WADA-COUNT-TABLE. 

           MOVE WS-OPEN-FULL-ADA-CABINS TO WADA-FULL-CABINS (OCC-SUB).  
           MOVE WS-OPEN-SSA-ADA-CABINS  TO WADA-SSA-CABINS (OCC-SUB).   
           MOVE WS-OPEN-PART-ADA-CABINS TO WADA-PART-CABINS (OCC-SUB).  

           GO TO 8205-NEXT-PHANTOM-CAT. 

       8210-CLOSE-META-CUR. 
           EXEC SQL CLOSE PHANTOM_META_CUR END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 82003 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           IF WS-LAST-CAT-CODE > SPACES 
              PERFORM 8250-GET-ADA-UPG-INFO THRU 8299-EXIT. 

           EXEC SQL DECLARE META_UPDATE_CUR CURSOR FOR RETENTION
              SELECT META_CODE,SUM(OPEN_CABINS - OPEN_LOGICAL_ADJUST),  
                     SUM(OPEN_FULL_ADA_CABINS + OPEN_SSA_ADA_CABINS +   
                         OPEN_PART_ADA_CABINS - OPEN_LOGICAL_ADA_ADJUST)
                 FROM SAIL_OPEN_INVENTORY SOI   
                 WHERE SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE 
                   AND SAIL_ID = :TDP01-SAIL-ID 
                   AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM SAIL_CATEGORY SC   
                                      WHERE SC.SHIP_CODE = SOI.SHIP_CODE
                                        AND SC.SAIL_ID = SOI.SAIL_ID
                                        AND SC.CATEGORY_CODE =  
                                            SOI.CATEGORY_CODE   
                                        AND SC.PHANTOM_CAT_IND = 'Y')   
                 GROUP BY META_CODE 
           END-EXEC.

           EXEC SQL OPEN META_UPDATE_CUR END-EXEC.  

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 82004 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           EXEC SQL WHENEVER NOT FOUND GO TO 8220-FINISH-META-UPDATE
           END-EXEC.

       8215-NEXT-META-UPDATE.   
           EXEC SQL FETCH NEXT META_UPDATE_CUR  
              INTO WS-META-CODE 
                  ,WS-TOT-OPEN-CABINS   
                  ,WS-TOT-OPEN-ADA-CABINS   
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 82005 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           EXEC SQL WHENEVER NOT FOUND CONTINUE END-EXEC.   

           EXEC SQL SELECT TOTAL_OPEN_CABINS,TOTAL_OPEN_ADA_CABINS  
              INTO WS-OLD-TOT-CABINS,WS-OLD-TOT-ADA-CABINS  
              FROM SAIL_META
              WHERE SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE
                AND SAIL_ID = :TDP01-SAIL-ID
                AND META_CODE = :WS-META-CODE   
              FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
           END-EXEC 

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 82006 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           IF WS-OLD-TOT-CABINS = WS-TOT-OPEN-CABINS AND
                 WS-OLD-TOT-ADA-CABINS = WS-TOT-OPEN-ADA-CABINS 
              GO TO 8215-NEXT-META-UPDATE.  

           IF (WS-OLD-TOT-CABINS NOT = WS-TOT-OPEN-CABINS)  
              MOVE 'SAIL_META Total Cabins <> TOTAL' TO WS-DET-MESSAGE  
              PERFORM 8000-WRITE-PRINT-REC THRU 8099-EXIT.  

           IF (WS-OLD-TOT-ADA-CABINS NOT = WS-TOT-OPEN-ADA-CABINS)  
              MOVE 'SAIL_META Total ADA Cabins <> TOTAL' TO 
                   WS-DET-MESSAGE   
              PERFORM 8000-WRITE-PRINT-REC THRU 8099-EXIT.  

           EXEC SQL UPDATE SAIL_META
              SET TOTAL_OPEN_CABINS = :WS-TOT-OPEN-CABINS   
                 ,TOTAL_OPEN_ADA_CABINS = :WS-TOT-OPEN-ADA-CABINS   
              WHERE SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE
                AND SAIL_ID = :TDP01-SAIL-ID
                AND META_CODE = :WS-META-CODE   
                AND (TOTAL_OPEN_CABINS <> :WS-TOT-OPEN-CABINS   
                 OR  TOTAL_OPEN_ADA_CABINS <> :WS-TOT-OPEN-ADA-CABINS)  
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 82007 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           GO TO 8215-NEXT-META-UPDATE. 

       8220-FINISH-META-UPDATE. 
           EXEC SQL CLOSE META_UPDATE_CUR END-EXEC. 

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 82008 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

       8249-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

       8250-GET-ADA-UPG-INFO.   
           EXEC SQL DECLARE ADA_FULL_UPG_CUR CURSOR FOR RETENTION   
              SELECT OCCUPANCY,SUM(OPEN_FULL_ADA_CABINS)
                 FROM SAIL_CATEGORY SC  
                 JOIN SAIL_OPEN_INVENTORY SOI   
                   ON SOI.SHIP_CODE = SC.SHIP_CODE  
                  AND SOI.SAIL_ID = SC.SAIL_ID  
                  AND SOI.CATEGORY_CODE = SC.CATEGORY_CODE  
                  AND SOI.RATE_CODE = '~'   
                  AND SOI.GROUP_BOOK_NUM = '~'  
                 WHERE SC.SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE  
                   AND SC.SAIL_ID = :TDP01-SAIL-ID  
                   AND SC.PHANTOM_CAT_IND = 'N' 
                   AND EXISTS   
                       (SELECT 1
                           FROM SAIL_OPEN_INVENTORY SOIA
                           WHERE SOIA.SHIP_CODE = SC.SHIP_CODE  
                             AND SOIA.SAIL_ID = SC.SAIL_ID  
                             AND SOIA.META_CODE = :WS-LAST-META-CODE
                             AND SOIA.OCCUPANCY = SOI.OCCUPANCY 
                             AND SOIA.ADA_FULL_UPGRADE_CAT_CODE =   
                                 SC.CATEGORY_CODE)  
              GROUP BY OCCUPANCY
           END-EXEC.

           EXEC SQL OPEN ADA_FULL_UPG_CUR END-EXEC. 

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 82009 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           EXEC SQL WHENEVER NOT FOUND GO TO 8260-CLOSE-FULL-UPG
           END-EXEC.

       8255-NEXT-FULL-UPG.  
           EXEC SQL FETCH NEXT ADA_FULL_UPG_CUR 
              INTO OSUB 
                  ,WS-UPGRADE-ADA-CABINS
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 82010 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           EXEC SQL WHENEVER NOT FOUND CONTINUE END-EXEC.   

           ADD WS-UPGRADE-ADA-CABINS TO WADA-FULL-CABINS (OSUB).

           GO TO 8255-NEXT-FULL-UPG.

       8260-CLOSE-FULL-UPG. 
           EXEC SQL CLOSE ADA_FULL_UPG_CUR END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 82011 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           EXEC SQL DECLARE ADA_SSA_UPG_CUR CURSOR FOR RETENTION
              SELECT OCCUPANCY,SUM(OPEN_SSA_ADA_CABINS) 
                 FROM SAIL_CATEGORY SC  
                 JOIN SAIL_OPEN_INVENTORY SOI   
                   ON SOI.SHIP_CODE = SC.SHIP_CODE  
                  AND SOI.SAIL_ID = SC.SAIL_ID  
                  AND SOI.CATEGORY_CODE = SC.CATEGORY_CODE  
                  AND SOI.RATE_CODE = '~'   
                  AND SOI.GROUP_BOOK_NUM = '~'  
                 WHERE SC.SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE  
                   AND SC.SAIL_ID = :TDP01-SAIL-ID  
                   AND SC.PHANTOM_CAT_IND = 'N' 
                   AND EXISTS   
                       (SELECT 1
                           FROM SAIL_OPEN_INVENTORY SOIA
                           WHERE SOIA.SHIP_CODE = SC.SHIP_CODE  
                             AND SOIA.SAIL_ID = SC.SAIL_ID  
                             AND SOIA.META_CODE = :WS-LAST-META-CODE
                             AND SOIA.OCCUPANCY = SOI.OCCUPANCY 
                             AND SOIA.ADA_SSA_UPGRADE_CAT_CODE =
                                 SC.CATEGORY_CODE)  
              GROUP BY OCCUPANCY
           END-EXEC.

           EXEC SQL OPEN ADA_SSA_UPG_CUR END-EXEC.  

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 82012 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           EXEC SQL WHENEVER NOT FOUND GO TO 8270-CLOSE-SSA-UPG 
           END-EXEC.

       8265-NEXT-SSA-UPG.   
           EXEC SQL FETCH NEXT ADA_SSA_UPG_CUR  
              INTO OSUB 
                  ,WS-UPGRADE-ADA-CABINS
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 82013 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           EXEC SQL WHENEVER NOT FOUND CONTINUE END-EXEC.   

           ADD WS-UPGRADE-ADA-CABINS TO WADA-SSA-CABINS (OSUB). 

           GO TO 8265-NEXT-SSA-UPG. 

       8270-CLOSE-SSA-UPG.  
           EXEC SQL CLOSE ADA_SSA_UPG_CUR END-EXEC. 

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 82014 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           EXEC SQL DECLARE ADA_PART_UPG_CUR CURSOR FOR RETENTION   
              SELECT OCCUPANCY,SUM(OPEN_PART_ADA_CABINS)
                 FROM SAIL_CATEGORY SC  
                 JOIN SAIL_OPEN_INVENTORY SOI   
                   ON SOI.SHIP_CODE = SC.SHIP_CODE  
                  AND SOI.SAIL_ID = SC.SAIL_ID  
                  AND SOI.CATEGORY_CODE = SC.CATEGORY_CODE  
                  AND SOI.RATE_CODE = '~'   
                  AND SOI.GROUP_BOOK_NUM = '~'  
                 WHERE SC.SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE  
                   AND SC.SAIL_ID = :TDP01-SAIL-ID  
                   AND SC.PHANTOM_CAT_IND = 'N' 
                   AND EXISTS   
                       (SELECT 1
                           FROM SAIL_OPEN_INVENTORY SOIA
                           WHERE SOIA.SHIP_CODE = SC.SHIP_CODE  
                             AND SOIA.SAIL_ID = SC.SAIL_ID  
                             AND SOIA.META_CODE = :WS-LAST-META-CODE
                             AND SOIA.OCCUPANCY = SOI.OCCUPANCY 
                             AND SOIA.ADA_PART_UPGRADE_CAT_CODE =   
                                 SC.CATEGORY_CODE)  
              GROUP BY OCCUPANCY
           END-EXEC.

           EXEC SQL OPEN ADA_PART_UPG_CUR END-EXEC. 

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 82015 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           EXEC SQL WHENEVER NOT FOUND GO TO 8280-CLOSE-PART-UPG
           END-EXEC.

       8275-NEXT-PART-UPG.  
           EXEC SQL FETCH NEXT ADA_PART_UPG_CUR 
              INTO OSUB 
                  ,WS-UPGRADE-ADA-CABINS
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 82016 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           EXEC SQL WHENEVER NOT FOUND CONTINUE END-EXEC.   

           ADD WS-UPGRADE-ADA-CABINS TO WADA-PART-CABINS (OSUB).

           GO TO 8275-NEXT-PART-UPG.

       8280-CLOSE-PART-UPG. 
           EXEC SQL CLOSE ADA_PART_UPG_CUR END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 82017 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           MOVE ZERO TO OSUB.   

       8285-UPDATE-NEXT-OCCUPANCY.  
           ADD 1 TO OSUB.   

           IF OSUB > 5  
              GO TO 8299-EXIT.  

           EXEC SQL SELECT OPEN_FULL_ADA_CABINS 
                          ,OPEN_SSA_ADA_CABINS  
                          ,OPEN_PART_ADA_CABINS 
              INTO WS-OLD-FULL-ADA-CABINS   
                  ,WS-OLD-SSA-ADA-CABINS
                  ,WS-OLD-PART-ADA-CABINS   
              FROM SAIL_OPEN_INVENTORY  
              WHERE SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE
                AND SAIL_ID = :TDP01-SAIL-ID
                AND OCCUPANCY = :OSUB   
                AND CATEGORY_CODE = :WS-LAST-CAT-CODE   
                AND RATE_CODE = '~' 
                AND GROUP_BOOK_NUM = '~'
              FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 82018 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           IF WS-OLD-FULL-ADA-CABINS = WADA-FULL-CABINS (OSUB) AND  
                 WS-OLD-SSA-ADA-CABINS = WADA-SSA-CABINS (OSUB) AND 
                 WS-OLD-PART-ADA-CABINS = WADA-PART-CABINS (OSUB)   
              GO TO 8285-UPDATE-NEXT-OCCUPANCY. 

           IF (WS-OLD-FULL-ADA-CABINS NOT = WADA-FULL-CABINS (OSUB))
              MOVE SPACES TO WS-DET-MESSAGE 
              STRING 'Full ADA CNT MISMATCH ' OSUB DELIMITED BY SPACES  
                     INTO WS-DET-MESSAGE
              PERFORM 8000-WRITE-PRINT-REC THRU 8099-EXIT.  

           IF (WS-OLD-SSA-ADA-CABINS NOT = WADA-SSA-CABINS (OSUB))  
              MOVE SPACES TO WS-DET-MESSAGE 
              STRING 'SSA ADA CNT MISMATCH ' OSUB DELIMITED BY SPACES   
                     INTO WS-DET-MESSAGE
              PERFORM 8000-WRITE-PRINT-REC THRU 8099-EXIT.  

           IF (WS-OLD-PART-ADA-CABINS NOT = WADA-PART-CABINS (OSUB))
              MOVE SPACES TO WS-DET-MESSAGE 
              STRING 'Part ADA CNT MISMATCH ' OSUB DELIMITED BY SPACES  
                     INTO WS-DET-MESSAGE
              PERFORM 8000-WRITE-PRINT-REC THRU 8099-EXIT.  

           EXEC SQL UPDATE SAIL_OPEN_INVENTORY  
              SET OPEN_FULL_ADA_CABINS = :WADA-FULL-CABINS (OSUB)   
                 ,OPEN_SSA_ADA_CABINS = :WADA-SSA-CABINS (OSUB) 
                 ,OPEN_PART_ADA_CABINS = :WADA-PART-CABINS (OSUB)   
              WHERE SHIP_CODE = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE
                AND SAIL_ID = :TDP01-SAIL-ID
                AND OCCUPANCY = :OSUB   
                AND CATEGORY_CODE = :WS-LAST-CAT-CODE   
                AND RATE_CODE = '~' 
                AND GROUP_BOOK_NUM = '~'
                AND (OPEN_FULL_ADA_CABINS <> :WADA-FULL-CABINS (OSUB)   
                 OR  OPEN_SSA_ADA_CABINS <> :WADA-SSA-CABINS (OSUB) 
                 OR  OPEN_PART_ADA_CABINS <>
                     :WADA-PART-CABINS (OSUB))  
           END-EXEC.

           IF SQLCODE < 0   
              MOVE 82019 TO DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX
              GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR.

           GO TO 8285-UPDATE-NEXT-OCCUPANCY.

       8299-EXIT.   
           EXIT.

980649 8300-GRP-SOI-SOP-DATA.   
980649  
980649     MOVE SPACES               TO WS-CHECK-AN-FIELDS. 
980649     MOVE LOW-VALUES           TO WS-CHECK-N-FIELDS.  
980649     MOVE RSP15-O-CAT-CODE(RATE-SUB WS-PPR-SUB)   
980649                               TO WS-CHK-CAT-CODE.
980649  
980649     IF  RSP15-O-CPA-CAT-CODE(RATE-SUB WS-PPR-SUB)  > SPACES  
980649         MOVE RSP15-O-CPA-CAT-CODE(RATE-SUB WS-PPR-SUB)   
980649                               TO WS-CHK-CPA-CAT-CODE 
980649     ELSE 
980649         MOVE WS-CHK-CAT-CODE  TO WS-CHK-CPA-CAT-CODE 
980649     END-IF.  
980649  
980649     PERFORM VARYING CSUB FROM 1 BY 1 
980649               UNTIL CSUB > TDP01-SAIL-CAT-CNT
980649        IF TDP01-CATEGORY-CODE (CSUB) = WS-CHK-CPA-CAT-CODE   
980649           MOVE TDP01-SORT-KEY (CSUB) 
980649                               TO WS-CHK-CPA-SORT-KEY 
980649           MOVE TDP01-META-CODE (CSUB)
980649                               TO WS-CHK-CPA-META-CODE
980649        END-IF
980649        IF TDP01-CATEGORY-CODE (CSUB) = WS-CHK-CAT-CODE   
980649           MOVE TDP01-SORT-KEY (CSUB) 
980649                               TO WS-CHK-CAT-SORT-KEY 
980649           MOVE TDP01-META-CODE (CSUB)
980649                               TO WS-CHK-CAT-META-CODE
980649        END-IF
980649     END-PERFORM. 
980649  
980649     MOVE RSP15-O-OFFER-ID(RATE-SUB)  
980649                               TO WS-CHK-OFFER-ID.
980649     MOVE RSP15-OFFER-CODE(RATE-SUB)  
980649                               TO WS-CHK-OFFER-CODE.  
980649  
980649     MOVE 'N'                  TO WS-GRP-OFF-FND. 
980649  

980649     PERFORM VARYING GSUB1 FROM 1 BY 1
980649               UNTIL GSUB1 > 90   
980649         PERFORM VARYING OG-SUB1 FROM 1 BY 1  
980649             UNTIL OG-SUB1 > RSP15-O-OI-OFF-GUEST-CNT (GSUB1) 
980649             IF  (RSP15-O-OG-OFFER-ID (GSUB1 OG-SUB1) =   
980649                  WS-CHK-OFFER-ID )                            AND
980649                 (RSP15-O-OG-POSITION-NUM (GSUB1 OG-SUB1) = 0) AND
980649                 (RSP15-O-OG-CATEGORY-CODE(GSUB1 OG-SUB1) =   
980649                  WS-CHK-CPA-CAT-CODE)
980649                 MOVE 'Y'         TO WS-GRP-OFF-FND   
980649                 MOVE OG-SUB1     TO OG-SUB   
980649                 MOVE GSUB1       TO GSUB 
980649                 MOVE RSP15-O-OI-OFF-GUEST-CNT (GSUB1)
980649                                  TO OG-SUB1  
980649             END-IF   
980649         END-PERFORM  
980649         IF  WS-GRP-OFF-FND = 'Y' 
980649             MOVE 90              TO GSUB1
980649         END-IF   
980649     END-PERFORM. 
980649  
980649     IF  WS-GRP-OFF-FND NOT = 'Y'

980649  
980649     EXEC SQL 
980649        SELECT 'Y' INTO :WS-EXIST-IND 
980649          FROM SAIL_OFFER_PRICE   
980649         WHERE SHIP_CODE    = :TDP01-SHIP-CODE
980649           AND SAIL_ID      = :TDP01-SAIL-ID  
PRCCMB*          AND ACTIVE_IND IN ('Y','N')
980649           AND OFFER_CODE   = :WS-CHK-OFFER-CODE  
980649           AND OFFER_ID     = :WS-CHK-OFFER-ID
PRCCMB*          AND CPA_SORT_KEY = :WS-CHK-CPA-SORT-KEY
PRCCMB*          AND CAT_SORT_KEY = :WS-CHK-CAT-SORT-KEY
PRCCMB           AND CATEGORY_CODE = :WS-CHK-CAT-CODE   
PRCCMB           AND CPA_CATEGORY_CODE = :WS-CHK-CPA-CAT-CODE   
980649           AND REQUEST_CABIN_CODE = :WS-CHK-REQ-CABIN-CODE
980649        FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY
980649     END-EXEC.
980649  
980649     IF SQLCODE < 0   
980649        GO TO 9150-RDMS-ERROR 
980649     ELSE

980649           GO TO 8430-INSERT-SAIL-OFFER-PRICE 
980649        END-IF
980649     END-IF.

       9100-DATABASE-ERROR. 

           DISPLAY ' CURRENT SAILING: ' TDP01-SHIP-CODE 
                   TDP01-DURATION-DAYS ' ' TDP01-SAIL-DATE-YYYYMMDD 
                   UPON PRINTER.
           DISPLAY ' GROUP BOOK NUM : ' GBR1-BOOK-NUM UPON PRINTER. 
           DISPLAY ' INDV  BOOK NUM : ' IBR-BOOK-NUM UPON PRINTER.  
           DISPLAY ' CABIN NUMBER   : ' AVL-CAB-CABIN-NUM UPON PRINTER. 

           IF RB-ERROR-CODE NOT = '02'  
              GO TO DPP12-DATABASE-ERROR.   

           ADD 1 TO WS-DEADLOCK-CNT.

           IF WS-DEADLOCK-CNT > 10  
              GO TO DPP12-DATABASE-ERROR.   

           DISPLAY 'DEADLOCK  -- ATTEMPTING TO RECOVER '
                   WS-DEADLOCK-CNT ' TIME(S)'     UPON PRINTER. 
           DISPLAY 'DEADLOCK  -- ATTEMPTING TO RECOVER '
                   WS-DEADLOCK-CNT ' TIME(S)'.  

           ADD 30            TO WS-TOTAL-WAIT-TIME. 

           DISPLAY '----WAITING 30 SECONDS' UPON PRINTER.   
           DISPLAY '----WAITING 30 SECONDS'.

783211*    MOVE 30000 TO WS-WAIT-MILLISECONDS   
783211     MOVE 15000 TO WS-WAIT-MILLISECONDS   

           CALL 'ERTWAIT' USING WS-WAIT-MILLISECONDS

           DISPLAY 'CONTINUING AFTER WAITING'.  

           GO TO 0100-INIT-DATABASE.

       9150-RDMS-ERROR. 

           DISPLAY 'ERROR-MSG-CODE : ' DPP42-HOLD-MSG-INDEX 
                   UPON PRINTER.


           IF SQLSTATE >= '40000' AND SQLSTATE <= '40003'   
197506        OR IMPART-DEPART = 2  
              ADD 1 TO WS-DEADLOCK-CNT  

              IF WS-DEADLOCK-CNT > 10   
                 GO TO DPP12-RDMS-ERROR 
              END-IF

              DISPLAY 'DEADLOCK  -- ATTEMPTING TO RECOVER ' 
                      WS-DEADLOCK-CNT ' TIME(S)'     UPON PRINTER   
              DISPLAY 'DEADLOCK  -- ATTEMPTING TO RECOVER ' 
                      WS-DEADLOCK-CNT ' TIME(S)'

              ADD 30            TO WS-TOTAL-WAIT-TIME   

              DISPLAY '----WAITING 30 SECONDS' UPON PRINTER 
              DISPLAY '----WAITING 30 SECONDS'  

              MOVE 30000 TO WS-WAIT-MILLISECONDS

              CALL 'ERTWAIT' USING WS-WAIT-MILLISECONDS 

              DISPLAY 'CONTINUING AFTER WAITING' UPON PRINTER   
              DISPLAY 'CONTINUING AFTER WAITING'

              IF IMPART-DEPART = 1  
                 DEPART WITH ROLLBACK   
              END-IF

              GO TO 0100-INIT-DATABASE  
           END-IF.  

           GO TO DPP12-RDMS-ERROR.
